export const demoCoreFiles = [
  coreResources.map,coreResources.blocks,coreResources.bsp,coreResources.floor,
  coreResources.sky,coreResources.cab,coreResources.door,...coreResources.wallBanks,
  'QDEMO.EXE','CITYOBJ.SPR','BLAMMOG.SPR','CITYGUN.SPR','ETRACERS.SPR','EMISSILE.SPR','MFLASH.SPR',
  'TEXTURE1.DAT','ANIM1.DAT','DATA1.DAT','OBJ1.DAT','WHEELSPR.SPR',
];

export async function loadDemoCore(getFile) {
  const entries = await Promise.all(demoCoreFiles.map(async name => [name,await getFile(name)]));
  const files = new Map(entries);
  const image = await files.get('QDEMO.EXE').arrayBuffer();
  const data = new DataView(image,0x67200); // QDEMO LE object 2
  const words = (offset,count) => Array.from({length:count},(_,i) => data.getInt16(offset+2*i,true));
  const rows = (offset,count,width) => Array.from({length:count},(_,i) => words(offset+2*i*width,width));
  function sprites(offset,count) {
    return Array.from({length:count},(_,i) => {
      const descriptor = data.getUint32(offset+4*i,true);
      return [data.getUint8(descriptor+4),data.getInt8(descriptor+5),data.getInt8(descriptor+6)];
    });
  }
  const district = coreResources.district;
  const [cellX,cellY,z] = words(0x76f4+6*district,3);
  const [scaleX,scaleY,divisorX,divisorY] = Array.from({length:4},(_,i) => data.getInt32(0x61d4+4*i,true));
  const [offsetX,offsetY] = words(0x61e4+4*district,2);
  const start = {...coreResources,x:cellX*512+256,y:cellY*512+256,z,
    nitroCharges:data.getUint16(0x7ad0,true),
    shadeDistance:data.getInt16(0x7c24,true),shadeWeights:words(0x7c26,16),
    thrust:words(0x52fa,18),landingOffsets:words(0x77e4,16),
    mapDisplay:{...coreResources.mapDisplay,scaleX,scaleY,divisorX,divisorY,offsetX,offsetY,
      headingIcons:words(0x6204,8).map(index => 120+index),arrowPositions:rows(0x7524,8,2),
      distanceScale:data.getFloat64(0x1a5b,true)},
    mineOffsets:words(0x7764,16),shotOffsets:words(0x7784,16),
    actorScan:{
      columns:words(0x682c,21),offsets:rows(0x67dc,20,2),lanes:rows(0x6860,6,2),
      roadClasses:words(0x6898,128),random:new Uint8Array(image,0x67200+0x93dc,256).slice(),
      mineSelectors:[1,2,3].map(i => data.getInt16(0x6294+12*i,true)),
      gunBlocks:[102,103,104,105,117],launcherBlocks:[20,21,22,23,123],
      launchPorts:rows(0x5de8,10,3),sweeps:rows(0x5cdc,4,17),
      gunViews:[0x5b88,0x5ac8].flatMap(table => sprites(table,16).map(([image,x,y],i) => {
        const descriptor = data.getUint32(table+4*i,true);
        return [data.getUint32(descriptor,true) === 0x7bac,image,x,y];
      })),
      tracerImages:sprites(0x5be8,9).map(([image]) => image),
      missileViews:sprites(0x6bc0,9),blastOffsets:sprites(0x8dc8,12).map(([,x,y]) => [x,y]),
    }};
  const worker = new Worker(new URL('./district-worker.js',import.meta.url),{type:'module'});
  try {
    return await new Promise((resolve,reject) => {
      worker.onerror = event => reject(new Error(event.message || 'Unable to load demo Core assets.'));
      worker.onmessageerror = () => reject(new Error('Unable to transfer demo Core assets.'));
      worker.onmessage = ({data}) => data.error ? reject(new Error(data.error)) : resolve({...data.value,start,mineDamage:4,missileDamage:15});
      worker.postMessage({part:'demoCore',start,files});
    });
  } finally {
    worker.terminate();
  }
}

import {indexedDisplay, readScreen, readSpriteBank, retraces} from './indexed-screen.js';
import {coreResources, demoCoreFiles, loadDemoCore} from './demo-data.js';
import {prepareCabPipelines} from './cab-view.js';
import {newDriver} from './driving.js';
import {loadDrivingSounds} from './driving-audio.js';
import {loadHDImage} from './hd-images.js';
import {loadStarCubemap} from './skybox.js';
import {enterCab} from './menu.js';

export const demoRequiredFiles = ['LAKESHORE.MP4','QTITLE.GIF','QCREDIT2.GIF','CLICKER.VOC','WAYP2.SPR', ...demoCoreFiles];

function playIntro(video, source, signal) {
  return new Promise((resolve,reject) => {
    const fail = () => reject(new Error('Unable to play lakeshore.mp4.'));
    video.addEventListener('ended',resolve,{once:true,signal});
    video.addEventListener('error',fail,{once:true,signal});
    signal.addEventListener('abort',() => reject(signal.reason),{once:true});
    video.muted = false;
    video.src = source;
    video.hidden = false;
    video.play().catch(async error => {
      if (signal.aborted) return;
      if (error.name !== 'NotAllowedError') { fail(); return; }
      // Most browsers allow muted autoplay without a user gesture.
      video.muted = true;
      try { await video.play(); }
      catch (retry) {
        if (signal.aborted) return;
        if (retry.name !== 'NotAllowedError') { fail(); return; }
        const button = document.querySelector('#playDemoIntro');
        button.hidden = false;
        button.focus();
        button.addEventListener('click',() => {
          button.hidden = true;
          video.muted = false;
          video.play().catch(fail);
        },{once:true,signal});
      }
    });
  });
}

function loadingProgress() {
  const panel = document.querySelector('#demoLoading');
  const bar = document.querySelector('#demoProgress');
  const percent = document.querySelector('#demoPercent');
  const filename = document.querySelector('#demoLoadingFile');
  const pending = new Set();
  let total = 0, completed = 0, lastFile = '', visible = false;
  function render() {
    if (!visible) return;
    bar.value = total ? Math.floor(100*completed/total) : 0;
    percent.textContent = bar.value+'%';
    filename.textContent = pending.values().next().value ?? lastFile;
  }
  function track(name,work) {
    total++;
    pending.add(name);
    return work.then(result => {
      pending.delete(name);
      completed++;
      lastFile = name;
      render();
      return result;
    });
  }
  return {track,show() { visible = true; panel.hidden = false; render(); },hide() { visible = false; panel.hidden = true; }};
}

export async function runDemo({screen, getDisplay, bytes, getFile, getAudioTrack, audio, loadClick, launcher, introSource}) {
  const video = document.querySelector('#demoIntro');
  const events = new AbortController();
  const progress = loadingProgress();
  const {track} = progress;
  const objectURL = introSource instanceof Blob ? URL.createObjectURL(introSource) : null;
  launcher.hidden = true;
  try {
    const intro = playIntro(video,objectURL ?? introSource,events.signal);
    const displayReady = getDisplay();
    const titleReady = Promise.all([track('QTITLE.GIF',bytes('QTITLE.GIF')),
      track('HD/QTITLE.png',loadHDImage('QTITLE.GIF'))]).then(([raw,hd]) => ({...readScreen(raw,'QTITLE.GIF'),hd}));
    const trackedFile = (name,optional) => track(name,getFile(name,optional));
    const core = loadDemoCore(trackedFile);
    const soundsReady = loadDrivingSounds(audio,trackedFile,{engineName:null,nitroName:'NITRO.VOC'});
    const pipelinesReady = displayReady.then(prepareCabPipelines);
    const taxiReady = Promise.all([displayReady,pipelinesReady,import('./taxi-model.js')])
      .then(([display,pipelines,{loadTaxi}]) => loadTaxi(display.device,pipelines.taxi.materialLayout));
    const hdImage = name => track('HD/'+name.replace(/\.GIF$/i,'.png'),loadHDImage(name));
    // Register all startup jobs before presenting progress. File reads and
    // GPU/worker preparation can finish while the movie is still playing.
    const ready = Promise.all([
      track('CLICKER.VOC',loadClick()),track('WAYP2.SPR',bytes('WAYP2.SPR').then(raw => readSpriteBank(raw,37))),
      hdImage(coreResources.sky),hdImage(coreResources.cab),hdImage(coreResources.door),hdImage(coreResources.floor),
      track('night-cubemap.webp',loadStarCubemap()),track('driving-audio.js',soundsReady),
      track('district-worker.js',core),track('cab-view.js',pipelinesReady),track('taxi.glb',taxiReady),
      track('QCREDIT2.GIF',bytes('QCREDIT2.GIF').then(raw => readScreen(raw,'QCREDIT2.GIF'))),
    ]);
    // Observe background failures immediately and report them through the launcher.
    const loadingResult = Promise.allSettled([ready]);
    const [,title,display] = await Promise.all([intro,titleReady,displayReady]);
    const vga = indexedDisplay(display,9);
    vga.setImage(title);
    vga.present();
    screen.hidden = false;
    video.hidden = true;
    screen.focus();
    screen.setAttribute('aria-label','Quarantine demo title');
    progress.show();
    await vga.fade(title.dac,true);
    const [result] = await loadingResult;
    if (result.status === 'rejected') throw result.reason;
    const [click,wayfinder,hdSky,hdCab,hdDoor,hdFloor,starCubemap,sounds,data,pipelines,taxi,credits] = result.value;
    const district = {...data,hdSky,hdCab,hdDoor,hdFloor,starCubemap,
      taxiCenterZ:taxi?.centerZ,taxiBounds:taxi?.bounds};
    const session = {demo:true,district,pipelines,sounds,taxi,wayfinder,car:newDriver(district),welcome:false,resolution:Infinity};
    await vga.fade(title.dac,false);
    progress.hide();
    try {
      await enterCab({display,screen,getAudioTrack,bytes,audio,click},session);
    } finally {
      session.disc?.close();
    }
    screen.setAttribute('aria-label','Quarantine demo credits');
    vga.setImage(credits);
    await vga.fade(credits.dac,true);
    await retraces(180);
    await vga.fade(credits.dac,false);
  } finally {
    progress.hide();
    events.abort();
    document.querySelector('#playDemoIntro').hidden = true;
    video.hidden = true;
    video.pause();
    video.removeAttribute('src');
    video.load();
    if (objectURL) URL.revokeObjectURL(objectURL);
  }
}

import {createHDTexture} from './hd-images.js';
import {createPostprocess} from './postprocess.js';

// A fullscreen triangle presents the VGA frame and any replacement backdrop.
const width = 320, height = 200;
const movieHeight = 184;
const webgpuShader = `
@group(0) @binding(0) var pixels: texture_2d<u32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var colors: texture_2d<f32>;
struct Display { mode: u32, brightness: f32 };
@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> display: Display;
@group(0) @binding(3) var movie: texture_2d<u32>;
@group(0) @binding(4) var hdImage: texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(5) var coverage: texture_2d<u32>;

@vertex fn vertex(@builtin(vertex_index) id: u32) -> @builtin(position) vec4f {
  let p = vec2f(f32((id << 1u) & 2u), f32(id & 2u));
  return vec4f(p * 2.0 - vec2f(1.0), 0.0, 1.0);
}

@fragment fn fragment(@builtin(position) pos: vec4f) -> @location(0) vec4f {
  var xy = vec2u(pos.xy);
  if (display.mode == 2u) {
    let top = (${height}u - ${movieHeight}u) / 2u;
    if (xy.y < top || xy.y >= top + ${movieHeight}u) {
      return vec4f(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    }
    let rgb = textureLoad(movie, vec2i(vec2u(xy.x, xy.y - top)), 0).r;
    let channels = vec3u((rgb >> 10u) & 31u, (rgb >> 5u) & 31u, rgb & 31u);
    return vec4f(vec3f(channels) / 31.0, 1.0);
  }
  let hd = display.mode == 3u;
  if (hd) {
    xy = min(vec2u(pos.xy * vec2f(320,200) / vec2f(textureDimensions(hdImage))),vec2u(319,199));
  }
  if (display.mode == 0u || hd) {
    let addr = (xy.x & 3u) * 16000u + xy.y * 80u + (xy.x >> 2u);
    xy = vec2u(addr % 320u, addr / 320u);
  }
  let index = textureLoad(pixels, vec2i(xy), 0).r;
  let retail = textureLoad(colors, vec2i(i32(index), 0), 0);
  if (hd && textureLoad(coverage,vec2i(xy),0).r == 0u) {
    let replacement = textureLoad(hdImage,vec2i(pos.xy),0);
    return vec4f(mix(retail.rgb,replacement.rgb*display.brightness,replacement.a),1.0);
  }
  return retail;
}`;

export async function createDisplay(canvas) {
  if (!window.isSecureContext) throw new Error('WebGPU requires localhost or HTTPS.');
  const adapter = await navigator.gpu?.requestAdapter();
  if (!adapter) throw new Error('WebGPU is unavailable. Check graphics acceleration in Chrome or Brave.');
  const device = await adapter.requestDevice();
  const format = navigator.gpu.getPreferredCanvasFormat();
  const postprocess = await createPostprocess(device,format);
  const shader = device.createShaderModule({code: webgpuShader});
  const pipeline = await device.createRenderPipelineAsync({
    layout: 'auto', vertex: {module: shader, entryPoint: 'vertex'},
    fragment: {module: shader, entryPoint: 'fragment', targets: [{format}]},
    primitive: {topology: 'triangle-list'},
  });
  const makeTexture = (w, h, format) => device.createTexture({
    size: [w, h], format, usage: GPUTextureUsage.TEXTURE_BINDING | GPUTextureUsage.COPY_DST,
  });
  const pixels = makeTexture(width, height, 'r8uint');
  const colors = makeTexture(256, 1, 'rgba8unorm');
  const movie = makeTexture(width, movieHeight, 'r16uint');
  const coverage = makeTexture(width, height, 'r8uint');
  const fallback = makeTexture(1, 1, 'rgba8unorm');
  const layout = device.createBuffer({size: 16, usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST});
  const bind = hd => device.createBindGroup({layout: pipeline.getBindGroupLayout(0), entries: [
    {binding: 0, resource: pixels.createView()}, {binding: 1, resource: colors.createView()},
    {binding: 2, resource: {buffer: layout}}, {binding: 3, resource: movie.createView()},
    {binding: 4, resource: hd.createView()}, {binding: 5, resource: coverage.createView()},
  ]});
  const group = bind(fallback);
  const context = canvas.getContext('webgpu');
  if (!context) throw new Error('WebGPU canvas unavailable.');
  context.configure({device, format, alphaMode: 'opaque'});
  const fpsCounters = document.querySelectorAll('[data-fps]');
  let submittedFrames = 0, sampleTime = performance.now();
  function recordFrame() { submittedFrames++; }
  const fpsInterval = setInterval(() => {
    const now = performance.now(), elapsed = now-sampleTime;
    const text = Math.round(submittedFrames*1000/elapsed)+' FPS';
    for (const counter of fpsCounters) counter.textContent = text;
    submittedFrames = 0;
    sampleTime = now;
  },1000);
  const mode = new Uint32Array(4);
  const parameters = new Float32Array(mode.buffer);
  let previousMode, previousBrightness, currentBitmap, hdTexture, hdGroup;
  function draw(displayMode, brightness = 1) {
    const w = displayMode === 3 ? hdTexture.width : width;
    const h = displayMode === 3 ? hdTexture.height : height;
    const ow = w, oh = h;
    if (canvas.width !== ow) canvas.width = ow;
    if (canvas.height !== oh) canvas.height = oh;
    if (previousMode !== displayMode || previousBrightness !== brightness) {
      previousMode = displayMode;
      previousBrightness = brightness;
      mode[0] = displayMode;
      parameters[1] = brightness;
      device.queue.writeBuffer(layout, 0, mode);
    }
    const commands = device.createCommandEncoder();
    const output = context.getCurrentTexture().createView();
    postprocess.release();
    const pass = commands.beginRenderPass({colorAttachments: [{
      view: output, loadOp: 'clear', storeOp: 'store',
      clearValue: {r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 1},
    }]});
    pass.setPipeline(pipeline);
    pass.setBindGroup(0, displayMode === 3 ? hdGroup : group);
    pass.draw(3);
    pass.end();
    device.queue.submit([commands.finish()]);
    recordFrame();
  }

  const rgba = new Uint8Array(256 * 4).fill(255);
  return {
    device, context, format, postprocess, recordFrame,
    close() {
      clearInterval(fpsInterval);
      postprocess.close();
      hdTexture?.destroy();
      for (const texture of [pixels,colors,movie,coverage,fallback]) texture.destroy();
      layout.destroy();
      context.unconfigure();
      device.destroy();
    },
    presentIndexed(frame, dac, pixelsChanged, linear = false, paletteChanged = true, replacement) {
      if (replacement && currentBitmap !== replacement.bitmap) {
        currentBitmap = replacement.bitmap;
        hdTexture?.destroy();
        hdTexture = createHDTexture(device, currentBitmap);
        hdGroup = hdTexture && bind(hdTexture);
        pixelsChanged = true;
      }
      const hd = replacement && hdTexture;
      if (pixelsChanged) device.queue.writeTexture({texture: pixels}, frame, {bytesPerRow: width}, [width, height]);
      if (hd && pixelsChanged) {
        device.queue.writeTexture({texture: coverage}, replacement.coverage, {bytesPerRow: width}, [width, height]);
      }
      if (paletteChanged) {
        for (let i = 0, j = 0; i < dac.length; j += 4) {
          for (let channel = 0; channel < 3; channel++) {
            const value = dac[i++];
            rgba[j + channel] = (value << 2) | (value >> 4);
          }
        }
        device.queue.writeTexture({texture: colors}, rgba, {}, [256, 1]);
      }
      draw(hd ? 3 : Number(linear), replacement?.brightness);
    },
    presentAvi(frame) {
      device.queue.writeTexture({texture: movie}, frame, {bytesPerRow: width * 2}, [width, movieHeight]);
      draw(2);
    },
  };
}

// All file offsets below refer to the September 1994 retail SKIPIT.EXE.
export function readPromptTables(bytes) {
  const base = 0x7d600; // LE object 3 on disk, runtime base 0x80000
  if (bytes.length !== 572501 || bytes[0] !== 77 || bytes[1] !== 90) {
    throw new Error('Select the September 1994 retail version of SKIPIT.EXE.');
  }
  const view = new DataView(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength);
  const lines = [];
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  for (let id = 0; id <= 21; id++) {
    const offset = base + view.getUint32(base + 0xae58 + id * 4, true);
    const end = bytes.indexOf(0, offset);
    if (end < offset || end - offset > 100) throw new Error('Unrecognized SKIPIT.EXE prompt table.');
    lines[id] = decoder.decode(bytes.subarray(offset, end));
  }
  const advances = table => Array.from({length: 59}, (_, i) =>
    view.getUint16(base + table + (32 + i) * 2, true));
  const menuY = Array.from({length: 5}, (_, i) => view.getUint16(base + 0x64b0 + 2 * i, true));
  const storyStart = base + 0xb540;
  const storyEnd = bytes.indexOf(0, storyStart);
  if (storyEnd < storyStart || storyEnd >= base + 0xbd35) throw new Error('Invalid opening crawl.');
  const storyLines = decoder.decode(bytes.subarray(storyStart, storyEnd)).replace(/\n$/, '').split('\n');
  const stringAt = address => {
    const offset = base + view.getUint32(base + address, true);
    const end = bytes.indexOf(0, offset);
    if (end < offset || end - offset > 128) throw new Error('Invalid retail district string.');
    return decoder.decode(bytes.subarray(offset, end));
  };
  // dispatch_options_selection writes district 1 at 0x2AD3A.
  const district = 1;
  const asset = table => stringAt(table + district * 4).toUpperCase();
  const start = base + 0x8b20 + district * 6;
  const newGame = {
    district, map: asset(0x66b0), blocks: asset(0xadac), bsp:asset(0xadc4), floor: asset(0x6680), sky: asset(0x6698),
    // 0x4044F and 0x414C4 dereference a MAP id through this table. BLK
    // directory record zero lives at DS:27270, with 40 bytes per descriptor.
    blockRecords: Array.from({length:128}, (_,i) => {
      const pointer = view.getUint32(base+0xaba8+4*i,true);
      return pointer ? (pointer-0x27270)/40 : -1;
    }),
    viewCells: Array.from({length:49}, (_,i) => [
      view.getInt16(base+0xa9ba+4*i,true), view.getInt16(base+0xa9bc+4*i,true),
    ]),
    actorScan: {
      columns:Array.from({length:21},(_,i) => view.getInt16(base+0x60b4+2*i,true)),
      offsets:Array.from({length:20},(_,i) => [view.getInt16(base+0x6064+4*i,true),view.getInt16(base+0x6066+4*i,true)]),
      lanes:Array.from({length:6},(_,i) => [view.getInt16(base+0x614c+4*i,true),view.getInt16(base+0x614e+4*i,true)]),
      random:bytes.slice(base+0xaa7e,base+0xab7e),
      launcherBlocks:Array.from({length:5},(_,i) => view.getInt16(base+0x60f2+20*district+2*i,true)),
      launchPorts:Array.from({length:10},(_,i) => [0,2,4].map(k => view.getInt16(base+0xc688+6*i+k,true))),
      missileViews:Array.from({length:9},(_,i) => {
        const descriptor = base+view.getUint32(base+0x63e8+4*i,true);
        return [view.getUint8(descriptor+4),view.getInt8(descriptor+5),view.getInt8(descriptor+6)];
      }),
      gunBlocks:Array.from({length:4},(_,i) => view.getInt16(base+0x60e8+20*district+2*i,true)),
      sweeps:Array.from({length:4},(_,row) => Array.from({length:17},(_,i) =>
        view.getInt16(base+0xc580+2*(17*row+i),true))),
      blastOffsets:Array.from({length:12},(_,i) => {
        const descriptor = base+view.getUint32(base+0x9ee4+4*i,true);
        return [view.getInt8(descriptor+5),view.getInt8(descriptor+6)];
      }),
      mineSelectors:Array.from({length:3},(_,i) => view.getInt16(base+0x5d68+12*(i+1),true)),
    },
    panorama: bytes[base+0x97aa+district] !== 0,
    wallBanks: Array.from({length: 16}, (_, i) => stringAt(0x64e8 + district * 64 + i * 4).toUpperCase()),
    shadeWeights: Array.from({length:16}, (_, i) => view.getInt16(base+0x8c5a+2*i,true)),
    depthBands: bytes.slice(base+0x8c7a,base+0x8c7a+64),
    x: view.getInt16(start, true) * 512 + 256, y: view.getInt16(start + 2, true) * 512 + 256,
    z: view.getInt16(start + 4, true), heading: view.getInt16(base + 0x8b74 + district * 2, true),
    welcome: [stringAt(0x9e28), stringAt(0x9e2c)],
    thrust: Array.from({length: 18}, (_, i) => view.getInt16(base + 0x52a8 + i * 2, true)),
    sine: Array.from({length: 1441}, (_, i) => view.getInt16(base + 0x71a8 + i * 2, true)),
    // Landing selects row four of the 16-word vertical displacement patterns.
    mineOffsets:Array.from({length:16},(_,i) => view.getInt16(base+0x8b90+i*2,true)),
    shotOffsets:Array.from({length:16},(_,i) => view.getInt16(base+0x8b90+(16+i)*2,true)),
    landingOffsets: Array.from({length:16}, (_,i) => view.getInt16(base+0x8b90+(4*16+i)*2,true)),
    mapDisplay: {
      scaleX: view.getInt32(base + 0xc700, true), scaleY: view.getInt32(base + 0xc704, true),
      divisorX: view.getInt32(base + 0xc708, true), divisorY: view.getInt32(base + 0xc70c, true),
      offsetX: view.getInt16(base + 0xc710 + district * 4, true),
      offsetY: view.getInt16(base + 0xc712 + district * 4, true),
      headingIcons: Array.from({length:8}, (_,i) => 120+view.getUint16(base+0xc768+2*i,true)),
      bearings: Array.from({length:256}, (_,i) => view.getUint16(base+0x8868+2*i,true)),
      bayNames: ['',...Array.from({length:4}, (_,i) => stringAt(0xc7b8+i*4))],
      districtNames: Array.from({length:6}, (_,i) => stringAt(0xc7dc+i*4)),
      currentLocation: decoder.decode(bytes.subarray(base+0x3734,bytes.indexOf(0,base+0x3734))),
      distanceFormat: decoder.decode(bytes.subarray(base+0x3768,bytes.indexOf(0,base+0x3768))),
      distanceScale: view.getFloat64(base+0x3793,true),
      arrowPositions: Array.from({length:8}, (_,i) => [
        view.getInt16(base+0x70b2+i*4,true),view.getInt16(base+0x70b4+i*4,true),
      ]),
      firstColor: view.getUint16(base+0xc730+4*district,true),
      endColor: view.getUint16(base+0xc732+4*district,true),
      colors: Array.from({length:18}, (_,i) => view.getUint16(base+0xc744+2*i,true)),
    },
  };
  return { lines, storyLines, newGame, widths: advances(0xaee8), slotWidths: advances(0xae70), menuY, step: view.getUint16(base + 0x8c56, true) };
}

export function readQuestion(bytes, randomValue) {
  if (bytes.length !== 468 * 8) throw new Error('Invalid retail PW.ENC record table.');
  const fields = new DataView(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength);
  const start = (randomValue % 468) * 8;
  const word = i => fields.getUint16(start + i * 2, true) ^ 0x5555;
  return { speed: word(1), weight: word(2), answer: word(3) };
}

// The startup voices are Creative Voice type 1 blocks with mono 8-bit PCM.
export function readVoice(context, bytes, filename) {
  const data = new DataView(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength);
  const block = data.getUint16(20, true);
  const size = bytes[block + 1] + bytes[block + 2] * 256 + bytes[block + 3] * 65536;
  if (bytes[block] !== 1 || bytes[block + 5] !== 0 || size <= 2 || block + 4 + size > bytes.length) {
    throw new Error('Unsupported audio in ' + filename);
  }
  const rate = 1000000 / (256 - bytes[block + 4]);
  const buffer = context.createBuffer(1, size - 2, rate);
  const output = buffer.getChannelData(0);
  for (let i = 0; i < output.length; i++) output[i] = (bytes[block + 6 + i] - 128) / 128;
  return buffer;
}


// Watcom rand at 0x537BC. imul preserves the low 32 bits of the x86 product.
export function firstRand(seed) {
  return ((Math.imul(seed, 0x41c64e6d) + 12345) >>> 16) & 0x7fff;
}

// The comparisons at 0x47F14 use signed AX and integer division.
export function validateCopyAnswer(word, digits) {
  const answer = (word << 16) >> 16;
  return Math.trunc(answer / 100) === Number(digits[0]) &&
    Math.trunc(answer % 100 / 10) === Number(digits[1]) &&
    answer % 10 === Number(digits[2]);
}

import {readVoice} from './dos-assets.js';
import {effectsOutput} from './settings.js';

export async function loadDrivingSounds(context,getFile,{engineName = 'ENG7.VOC', nitroName} = {}) {
  async function load(name, optional = false) {
    try {
      const file = await getFile(name,true);
      if (!file && optional) return null;
      if (!file) throw new Error(name+' is missing.');
      return readVoice(context,new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer()),name);
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Unable to load driving effect '+name,error);
      return null;
    }
  }
  const [engine,impact,horn,arrival,blast1,blast2,launch,nitro] = await Promise.all([
    engineName ? load(engineName) : null,load('IMPACT.VOC'),load('HORN.VOC'),load('TING.VOC'),load('EXPLO1.VOC'),load('EXPLO2.VOC'),load('FIREBELC.VOC'),nitroName ? load(nitroName,true) : null,
  ]);
  return {engine,nitro,impact,horn,arrival,blasts:[blast1,blast2],launch,unavailable:[!launch && 'FIREBELC.VOC',!blast1 && 'EXPLO1.VOC',!blast2 && 'EXPLO2.VOC',!engine && engineName,!impact && 'IMPACT.VOC',!horn && 'HORN.VOC',!arrival && 'TING.VOC'].filter(Boolean)};
}

export function createDrivingAudio(context,sounds) {
  const output = context.createGain();
  output.gain.value = 0;
  output.connect(effectsOutput(context));
  let active = false, closed = false, motor, motorGain;
  let lastSpeed = -1;
  const oneShots = new Set();

  function releaseSource(source) {
    oneShots.delete(source);
    source.onended = null;
    source.disconnect();
  }

  function stopOneShots() {
    for (const source of oneShots) {
      releaseSource(source);
      source.stop();
    }
  }

  function setActive(enabled) {
    if (closed || active === enabled) return;
    active = enabled;
    if (enabled && !motor && sounds.engine) {
      motor = context.createBufferSource();
      motor.buffer = sounds.engine;
      motor.loop = true;
      motorGain = context.createGain();
      motorGain.gain.value = 0.30;
      motor.connect(motorGain).connect(output);
      motor.start();
    }
    output.gain.setTargetAtTime(enabled ? 1 : 0,context.currentTime,0.012);
    if (!enabled) stopOneShots();
  }

  function stopEngine() {
    motor?.stop();
    motor?.disconnect();
    motorGain?.disconnect();
    motor = motorGain = null;
    lastSpeed = -1;
  }

  function update(velocity) {
    const magnitude = Math.min(350,Math.abs(velocity));
    if (!active || !motor || magnitude === lastSpeed) return;
    lastSpeed = magnitude;
    // SKIPIT 0x4ACA6 writes the 16.16 step directly to the active sample.
    const step = 0x8ba2+Math.trunc(magnitude*0x745d/350);
    motor.playbackRate.setValueAtTime(step/65536*11000/motor.buffer.sampleRate,context.currentTime);
  }

  function playEffect(buffer) {
    if (!active || !buffer || context.state !== 'running') return;
    const source = context.createBufferSource();
    source.buffer = buffer;
    const rate = Math.trunc(buffer.sampleRate);
    source.playbackRate.value = Math.trunc(rate*65536/11000)*11000/(65536*buffer.sampleRate);
    source.connect(output);
    oneShots.add(source);
    source.onended = () => releaseSource(source);
    source.start();
  }

  return {setActive,stopEngine,update,nitro:() => playEffect(sounds.nitro),launch:() => playEffect(sounds.launch),explode:i => playEffect(sounds.blasts[i]),arrive:() => playEffect(sounds.arrival),contact:() => playEffect(sounds.impact),horn:() => playEffect(sounds.horn),close() {
    if (closed) return;
    closed = true;
    active = false;
    stopOneShots();
    stopEngine();
    output.disconnect();
  }};
}

import {tickDistrictHazards} from './district-hazards.js';
import {bearing,sine} from './bearing.js';
import {projectedStreetContact,resolveStreetContact} from './street-contact.js';
import {cabBody, chaseRange, rideHeight, supportRadius} from './cab-dimensions.js';
import {architectureZ,architectureXY,architectureScale,architectureCellSize,vehicleZ} from './architecture.js';

// DOS 0x1AD00 initializes the keyboard dynamics and 0x1AD98 integrates them.
// The browser fixes the DOS frame-rate divisor so refresh rate cannot change handling.
export const ticksPerSecond = 35;
const fullTurn = 5760, quarter = 1440;
const brakeAcceleration = Math.trunc(-976/ticksPerSecond)*64;
const steeringTurn = Math.trunc(1000/ticksPerSecond), steeringCenter = Math.trunc(1536/ticksPerSecond);
const yawDamping = Math.trunc(320/ticksPerSecond), gravityStep = Math.trunc(160*12/ticksPerSecond);
const landingDelay = Math.max(1,Math.trunc(12/ticksPerSecond));
// Windshield framing, measured from the dashboard and horizon at the default FOV.
const windshieldYaw = -10.5*Math.PI/180, windshieldPitch = 7*Math.PI/180;

export function selectCabView(car,side=0) {
  car.camera = 0;
  car.lookYaw = side ? side*Math.PI/2 : windshieldYaw;
  car.lookPitch = side ? 0 : windshieldPitch;
}

const cabinYawLimit = 100*Math.PI/180;
const cabinPitchLimit = 45*Math.PI/180;
const chasePitchLimit = 75*Math.PI/180;
const wrap = a => (a % fullTurn + fullTurn) % fullTurn;
// QDEMO 0x11662..0x1167A forms (4*i-i)*4 / measuredRate: 12*i/rate.
const demoRateAdjustedStep = index => index > 0 ? Math.max(1,Math.trunc(12*index/ticksPerSecond)) : 0;
const fallbackBounds = Object.freeze({left:-cabBody.width/2,right:cabBody.width/2,
  back:-cabBody.length/2,front:cabBody.length/2,top:cabBody.base-cabBody.height,bottom:cabBody.base});


export function newDriver({start}) {
  return {x: start.x, y: start.y, z: start.z, angle: start.heading*16,
    hazards:{mines:new Map(),guns:new Map(),shots:new Set(),phase:0,column:0,randomIndex:0},health:400,mineSound:null,
    supportZ:start.z, fallSpeed:0, hover:0, landingCounter:0, landingStep:0, landingDelay:0, seatOffset:0,
    demo:Boolean(start.demo), nitroCharges:start.nitroCharges ?? 0, nitroTicks:0,
    wayfinderPhase:0, headlights:true, streetContact:null,
    speed: 0, velocity: null, steering: 0, yawSlip:0, yawSlipRate:0, previousYawSlip:0, skidMode:false,
    camera: 0, lookYaw: windshieldYaw, lookPitch: windshieldPitch, chaseYaw: null, chaseIdle: 0,
    chasePitch: null, chaseFloorLook: 0, chaseDistance: chaseRange.middle};
}

// QDEMO 0x34B40 sets both the published velocity and its 8-bit fixed-point accumulator.
export function engageNitro(car) {
  if (!car.demo || car.nitroTicks || !car.nitroCharges) return false;
  car.nitroCharges--;
  car.nitroTicks = 40;
  car.velocity = 600;
  car.speed = 600*256;
  return true;
}

export function startHover(car,power=32) {
  if (car.hover) return;
  car.hover = -1;
  car.fallSpeed = power << 4;
  car.landingCounter = car.demo ? demoRateAdjustedStep(power >> 3) : 0;
}

function chaseLift(distance, centerZ, bottom) {
  const {near,middle,far} = chaseRange;
  const nearLift = centerZ-bottom+2;
  const farLift = 88+bottom-centerZ;
  return distance <= middle ? nearLift+(26-nearLift)*(distance-near)/(middle-near) :
    26+(farLift-26)*(distance-middle)/(far-middle);
}

function chasePitch(car, distance, centerZ, bottom) {
  const lowest = Math.atan2(centerZ-bottom+2,distance);
  const pitch = car.chasePitch ?? Math.atan2(chaseLift(distance,centerZ,bottom),distance);
  return Math.max(lowest,Math.min(chasePitchLimit,pitch));
}

export function turnView(car, movementX, movementY = 0, district) {
  const delta = -movementX*0.0025;
  if (car.camera) {
    car.chaseYaw = ((car.chaseYaw ?? car.angle*Math.PI/2880)+delta) % (2*Math.PI);
    if (movementX || movementY) car.chaseIdle = 0;
    if (movementY) {
      const distance = car.chaseDistance;
      const centerZ = district?.taxiCenterZ ?? (cabBody.base-cabBody.height/2);
      const bottom = (district?.taxiBounds ?? fallbackBounds).bottom;
      const pitch = chasePitch(car,distance,centerZ,bottom);
      const lowest = Math.atan2(centerZ-bottom+2,distance);
      let movement = movementY*0.0025;
      if (movement > 0 && car.chaseFloorLook > 0) {
        const returning = Math.min(movement,car.chaseFloorLook);
        car.chaseFloorLook -= returning;
        movement -= returning;
      }
      const next = pitch+movement;
      car.chaseFloorLook = Math.min(chasePitchLimit,(car.chaseFloorLook ?? 0)+Math.max(0,lowest-next));
      car.chasePitch = Math.max(lowest,Math.min(chasePitchLimit,next));
    }
  } else {
    car.lookYaw = Math.max(-cabinYawLimit,Math.min(cabinYawLimit,car.lookYaw+delta));
    car.lookPitch = Math.max(-cabinPitchLimit,Math.min(cabinPitchLimit,car.lookPitch+movementY*0.0025));
  }
}

export function updateChaseCamera(car, heading, elapsed, moving) {
  if (!car.camera) return;
  const previous = car.chaseIdle;
  car.chaseIdle += elapsed;
  if (!moving) return;
  const target = heading*Math.PI/2880;
  car.chaseYaw ??= target;
  const difference = Math.atan2(Math.sin(target-car.chaseYaw),Math.cos(target-car.chaseYaw));
  if (Math.abs(difference) < 0.0001) {
    car.chaseYaw = target;
    car.chaseIdle = 0;
    return;
  }
  const correctionTime = Math.max(0,car.chaseIdle-2)-Math.max(0,previous-2);
  if (correctionTime > 0) car.chaseYaw += difference*(1-Math.exp(-2.5*correctionTime));
}

export function zoomChase(car,pixels) {
  car.chaseDistance = Math.max(chaseRange.near,Math.min(chaseRange.far,
    car.chaseDistance*Math.exp(pixels*0.0018)));
}

// Ignore geometry entirely above the cab so overhead structures do not retract the boom.
function chaseClearance(district,x,y,z,dx,dy,dz,cabTop) {
  const margin = 2/Math.hypot(dx,dy,dz);
  let limit = 1+margin;
  if (dx < 0) limit = Math.min(limit,-x/dx);
  if (dx > 0) limit = Math.min(limit,(district.width*architectureCellSize-x)/dx);
  if (dy < 0) limit = Math.min(limit,-y/dy);
  if (dy > 0) limit = Math.min(limit,(district.height*architectureCellSize-y)/dy);
  const left = Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(x,x+dx)/architectureCellSize)-1);
  const right = Math.min(district.width-1,Math.floor(Math.max(x,x+dx)/architectureCellSize)+1);
  const top = Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(y,y+dy)/architectureCellSize)-1);
  const bottom = Math.min(district.height-1,Math.floor(Math.max(y,y+dy)/architectureCellSize)+1);
  for (let row=top; row<=bottom; row++) {
    for (let col=left; col<=right; col++) {
      const block = district.diagnostics.blocks[district.tiles[row*district.width+col]&127];
      const x0 = x-col*architectureCellSize, y0 = y-row*architectureCellSize;
      for (const {segment:[rawX,rawY,rawSX,rawSY,rawUpper,rawLower]} of block.wallRuns) {
        const wx=architectureXY(rawX), wy=architectureXY(rawY);
        const sx=architectureXY(rawSX), sy=architectureXY(rawSY);
        const upper=architectureZ(rawUpper), lower=architectureZ(rawLower);
        if (lower <= cabTop) continue;
        const det = dx*sy-dy*sx;
        if (!det) continue;
        const ax = wx-x0, ay = wy-y0;
        const t = (ax*sy-ay*sx)/det, u = (ax*dy-ay*dx)/det;
        const height = z+t*dz;
        if (t >= 0 && t <= limit && u >= 0 && u <= 1 && height >= upper && height <= lower) limit = t;
      }
      if (!dz) continue;
      for (const face of block.faces) {
        if (!face.floor) break; // BLK floors precede the walls.
        const [a] = face.vertices;
        if (!a || architectureZ(a[2]) < cabTop) continue;
        const t = (architectureZ(a[2])-z)/dz;
        if (t < 0 || t > limit) continue;
        const px = (x0+t*dx)/architectureScale, py = (y0+t*dy)/architectureScale;
        for (let k=2; k<face.vertices.length; k++) {
          const b = face.vertices[k-1], c = face.vertices[k];
          const ab = (b[0]-a[0])*(py-a[1])-(b[1]-a[1])*(px-a[0]);
          const bc = (c[0]-b[0])*(py-b[1])-(c[1]-b[1])*(px-b[0]);
          const ca = (a[0]-c[0])*(py-c[1])-(a[1]-c[1])*(px-c[0]);
          if (ab+bc+ca !== 0 && (Math.min(ab,bc,ca) >= 0 || Math.max(ab,bc,ca) <= 0)) {
            limit = t;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return Math.min(1,Math.max(0,limit-margin));
}

// Sweep points on the fitted body perimeter against zero-thickness wall runs.
function firstWallHit(district,x,y,dx,dy,zTop,zBottom) {
  let hit = null;
  function record(t,nx,ny) {
    if (t >= 0 && t <= 1 && (!hit || t < hit.t)) hit = {t,nx,ny};
  }
  const ex = x+dx, ey = y+dy;
  if (dx < 0 && ex < 0) record(-x/dx,1,0);
  if (dy < 0 && ey < 0) record(-y/dy,0,1);
  if (dx > 0 && ex >= district.width*512) record((district.width*512-x)/dx,-1,0);
  if (dy > 0 && ey >= district.height*512) record((district.height*512-y)/dy,0,-1);
  // Include owners on both sides of cell boundaries, even for an axial sweep.
  const left = Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(x,ex)/512)-1);
  const right = Math.min(district.width-1,Math.floor(Math.max(x,ex)/512)+1);
  const top = Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(y,ey)/512)-1);
  const bottom = Math.min(district.height-1,Math.floor(Math.max(y,ey)/512)+1);
  const {walls,wallOffsets,wallSides} = district;
  for (let cy=top; cy<=bottom; cy++) {
    for (let cx=left; cx<=right; cx++) {
      const tile = district.tiles[cy*district.width+cx];
      if (!(tile&127)) continue;
      const id = (tile&127)*4+((tile>>>8)&3);
      for (let at=wallOffsets[id]; at<wallOffsets[id+1]; at+=6) {
        if (zBottom <= walls[at+4] || zTop >= walls[at+5]) continue;
        const wx = walls[at+2], wy = walls[at+3], det = dx*wy-dy*wx;
        if (det === 0 || (det > 0 && !(wallSides[at/6]&2))) continue;
        const ax = cx*512+walls[at]-x, ay = cy*512+walls[at+1]-y;
        const u = (ax*dy-ay*dx)/det;
        if (u < 0 || u > 1) continue;
        const t = (ax*wy-ay*wx)/det;
        if (t < 0 || t > 1 || (hit && t >= hit.t)) continue;
        const length = Math.hypot(wx,wy);
        record(t,-wy/length,wx/length);
      }
    }
  }
  return hit;
}

// 0x2620A..0x26392 projects five endpoints from the proposed position
// along travel. The previous endpoints initially coincide at the spawn.
function sweepCab(car,district,dx,dy) {
  if (!dx && !dy) return null;
  const table = district.start.sine;
  const travel = district.start.mapDisplay?.bearings ?
    16*bearing(dy,dx,district.start.mapDisplay.bearings) :
    Math.round(Math.atan2(dx,dy)*2880/Math.PI);
  const endpoints = [];
  for (const offset of [-400,400,-208,208,0]) {
    const heading = travel+offset;
    const x = Math.floor(car.x+dx)+(120*sine(table,heading)>>11);
    const y = Math.floor(car.y+dy)+(120*sine(table,heading+quarter)>>11);
    const [px,py] = car.wallProbes?.[endpoints.length] ?? [Math.floor(car.x),Math.floor(car.y)];
    const hit = firstWallHit(district,px,py,x-px,y-py,car.z,car.z);
    if (hit) return hit;
    endpoints.push([x,y]);
  }
  car.wallProbes = endpoints;
  return null;
}

// 0x41484 tests the first and third BLK vertices as inclusive local bounds.
// Positive Z points down. A deck above the cab cannot support it from below.
function floorBelow(district,x,y,z) {
  const cx = x>>9, cy = y>>9;
  if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= district.width || cy >= district.height) return 0;
  let floor = 32000;
  const localX = x&511, localY = y&511;
  for (const [x0,y0,x1,y1,height] of district.supportFloors[district.tiles[cy*district.width+cx]&127]) {
    if (localX >= x0 && localX <= x1 && localY >= y0 && localY <= y1 && height >= z && height < floor) floor = height;
  }
  return floor === 32000 ? 0 : floor;
}

function ceilingAbove(district,x,y,z) {
  const cx = x>>9, cy = y>>9;
  if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= district.width || cy >= district.height) return null;
  const localX = x&511, localY = y&511;
  let ceiling = null;
  for (const [x0,y0,x1,y1,height] of district.ceilings?.[district.tiles[cy*district.width+cx]&127] ?? []) {
    if (localX >= x0 && localX <= x1 && localY >= y0 && localY <= y1 &&
        height-40 <= z && (ceiling === null || height-40 > ceiling)) ceiling = height-40;
  }
  return ceiling;
}

function followFloor(car,district) {
  // 0x317A1 uses the cab origin for these lift tiles, even with an active hover.
  const block = district.tiles[(car.y>>9)*district.width+(car.x>>9)] & 127;
  const level = district.start.district;
  if ((level === 3 && (block === 103 || block === 112)) || (level === 2 && block === 41)) {
    const x = car.x & 511, y = car.y & 511;
    if (x >= 8 && x <= 504 && y >= 8 && y <= 504) {
      const limit = level === 3 && block === 103 ? -92 : -200;
      if (car.z > limit) {
        car.z -= 2;
        if (car.z < limit) car.z = limit+10;
      }
      return;
    }
  }
  let support = floorBelow(district,car.x,car.y,car.z)-rideHeight;
  if (district.start.demo) {
    car.supportZ = support; // 0x347EE samples only the cell under the cab origin.
    // QDEMO builds DS:D4AC[i] as trunc(12*i/rate), with a minimum of one.
    const step = () => demoRateAdjustedStep(car.fallSpeed >> 6);
    if (car.hover < 0) {
      car.z -= step();
      car.fallSpeed -= 12 << 4;
      if (car.fallSpeed < 0) { car.hover = 1; car.fallSpeed = 0; }
    } else if (car.hover > 0) {
      car.z += step();
      if (car.fallSpeed < (48 << 4)) car.fallSpeed += 12 << 4;
      if (car.z >= support) {
        car.z = support;
        car.hover = 0;
        car.landingCounter--;
        if (car.landingCounter > 0) {
          car.fallSpeed = (car.landingCounter*2) << 4;
          if (!car.landingStep) car.landingStep = 1;
        }
      }
    } else if (car.z < support) {
      car.fallSpeed += 10 << 4;
      car.z = Math.min(support,car.z+step());
      if (car.z === support) car.fallSpeed = 0;
    } else {
      car.z = support;
      car.fallSpeed = 0;
    }
    return;
  }
  for (const offset of [-400,400,2480,3280]) {
    const heading = car.angle+offset;
    const x = Math.trunc(car.x)+(supportRadius*sine(district.start.sine,heading)>>11);
    const y = Math.trunc(car.y)+(supportRadius*sine(district.start.sine,heading+quarter)>>11);
    const height = floorBelow(district,x,y,car.z)-rideHeight;
    support = Math.min(support,height < car.supportZ-20 ? car.supportZ : height);
  }
  car.supportZ = support;
  if (car.hover < 0) {
    // 0x4168C uses 4*rawZ-20 for a reversed floor, 40 units above
    // the BLK render plane. 0x31B27 compares that value at the cab origin.
    const ceiling = ceilingAbove(district,car.x,car.y,car.z);
    if (ceiling !== null && ceiling !== 0 && car.z-ceiling < 50) {
      car.fallSpeed = 0;
      car.hover = 1;
      return;
    }
    const nextZ = car.z-(car.fallSpeed >> 6);
    car.z = nextZ;
    car.fallSpeed -= 192;
    if (car.fallSpeed < 0) {
      car.fallSpeed = 0;
      car.hover = 1;
    }
    return;
  }
  if (car.hover > 0) {
    car.z += car.fallSpeed >> 6;
    if (car.fallSpeed < 768) car.fallSpeed += 192;
    if (car.z >= support) {
      car.z = support;
      car.fallSpeed = 0;
      car.hover = 0;
      // The Z-key impulse sets the retail landing counter to 64. The first
      // touchdown clears active hover and arms pattern four if the seat is idle.
      if (!car.landingStep) car.landingStep = 1;
    }
    return;
  }
  if (car.z < support) {
    // DS:FAE0 is entry 160 of the rate-adjusted table built at 0x108BA.
    car.fallSpeed += gravityStep;
    car.z = Math.min(support,car.z+(car.fallSpeed>>6));
  } else {
    car.z = support;
  }
  if (car.z === support) car.fallSpeed = 0;
}

export function driveTick(car, district, keys) {
  if (car.nitroTicks > 0) {
    if (!car.hover && car.z < 10) car.z++;
    car.nitroTicks--;
  }
  const impact = moveCab(car,district,keys);
  followFloor(car,district);
  tickDistrictHazards(car,district,ticksPerSecond,firstWallHit);
  const offsets = district.start[car.seatEffect ?? 'landingOffsets'];
  car.seatOffset = 0;
  if (car.landingStep && offsets) {
    // QDEMO 0x34EE6 applies the signed displacement to player Z every tick.
    if (car.demo) car.z += offsets[car.landingStep];
    else car.seatOffset = offsets[car.landingStep];
    if (car.demo || --car.landingDelay < 0) {
      car.landingStep++;
      if (offsets[car.landingStep] === 99 || car.landingStep >= offsets.length) {
        car.landingStep = 0; car.seatEffect = null;
      }
      else if (!car.demo) car.landingDelay = landingDelay;
    }
  }
  const heading = car.angle+(car.yawSlip>>2);
  const table = district.start.sine;
  car.streetContact = projectedStreetContact(car,district,sine(table,heading),sine(table,heading+quarter));
  return impact;
}

function moveCab(car, district, keys) {
  const hz = ticksPerSecond;
  const speed = car.velocity ?? (car.speed >> 8);
  const table = district.start.sine;
  const alignment = Math.abs(sine(table,(car.yawSlip >> 2)+quarter) >> 3);
  if (keys.has('KeyW') || keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('Numpad8')) {
    const band = Math.max(0, Math.min(district.start.thrust.length-1, speed >> 5));
    const impulse = Math.trunc(alignment*district.start.thrust[band]/hz);
    car.speed += impulse;
    if (car.speed < 0) car.speed += impulse;
  } else if (keys.has('KeyS') || keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('Numpad2')) {
    car.speed += brakeAcceleration;
  } else {
    car.skidMode = false;
    car.speed -= Math.sign(car.speed)*Math.min(Math.abs(car.speed),256);
  }
  car.speed = Math.max(-100*256,car.speed);

  const turn = steeringTurn, center = steeringCenter;
  const alt = keys.has('AltLeft') || keys.has('AltRight');
  // Left has priority when both directions are down (0x1AF0C).
  if (!alt && (keys.has('KeyA') || keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('Numpad4'))) {
    car.steering = Math.min(480, car.steering+turn);
    if (car.steering < 0) car.steering = Math.min(0, car.steering+center);
  } else if (!alt && (keys.has('KeyD') || keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('Numpad6'))) {
    car.steering = Math.max(-480, car.steering-turn);
    if (car.steering > 0) car.steering = Math.max(0, car.steering-center);
  } else {
    car.steering -= Math.sign(car.steering)*Math.min(Math.abs(car.steering),center);
  }
  const velocity = car.speed >> 8;
  const oldAngle = car.angle;
  let heading = oldAngle;
  if (car.skidMode && car.steering) {
    car.yawSlipRate += Math.trunc(Math.trunc(3*car.steering*alignment/75)/hz) >> 4;
  } else if (velocity > 10) {
    const damping = yawDamping;
    car.yawSlipRate -= Math.sign(car.yawSlip)*damping;
    if ((car.previousYawSlip ^ car.yawSlip) < 0) {
      car.yawSlipRate >>= 1;
      if (car.yawSlipRate && Math.abs(car.yawSlipRate) < 2*damping) {
        car.yawSlipRate = 0;
        car.yawSlip = 0;
      }
    }
  } else {
    heading += car.yawSlip >> 2;
    car.yawSlip = 0;
    car.yawSlipRate = 0;
  }
  car.previousYawSlip = car.yawSlip;
  car.yawSlip += Math.trunc((car.yawSlipRate << 4)/hz);
  const slipLimit = 20 << 6;
  if (car.yawSlip > slipLimit) {
    heading += (car.yawSlip >> 2)-(20 << 4);
    car.yawSlip = slipLimit;
  } else if (car.yawSlip < -slipLimit) {
    heading += (car.yawSlip >> 2)+(20 << 4);
    car.yawSlip = -slipLimit;
  }
  const rotation = heading-oldAngle+(Math.trunc(velocity*car.steering/hz) >> 6);
  const newAngle = wrap(oldAngle+rotation);
  const sin = sine(table,newAngle), cos = sine(table,newAngle+quarter);
  const step = {velocity,dx:(Math.trunc(sin*velocity/hz)>>7)/8,dy:(Math.trunc(cos*velocity/hz)>>7)/8};
  resolveStreetContact(car,step,sin,cos,hz);
  // Class 9 changes the accumulator but leaves the published speed word intact.
  car.velocity = step.velocity;
  const {dx,dy} = step;
  if (!dx && !dy && !rotation) return 0;
  const hit = sweepCab(car,district,dx,dy);
  if (!hit) {
    car.x += dx; car.y += dy; car.angle = newAngle;
    return 0;
  }
  // A probe can swing across the cab on a reversal. Its contact fraction
  // is not a fraction of cab translation. Keep the last accepted origin.
  const dot = dx*hit.nx+dy*hit.ny;
  // The impact cone excludes both 45-degree endpoints (0x26435).
  const alongWall = dx*hit.ny-dy*hit.nx;
  const bearings = district.start.mapDisplay?.bearings;
  let headOn = Math.abs(dot) > Math.abs(alongWall);
  if (bearings && (dx || dy)) {
    // DOS compares the travel bearing with the wall bearing on the contacted side.
    const travel = bearing(dy,dx,bearings);
    const side = dot < 0 ? -1 : 1;
    const wall = bearing(side*hit.ny,side*hit.nx,bearings);
    const delta = Math.abs(travel-wall);
    headOn = Math.min(delta,360-delta) < 45;
  }
  if (headOn) {
    car.velocity = -(step.velocity >> 2);
    car.speed = car.velocity*256;
    car.skidMode = false;
  } else {
    const tx = (dx-dot*hit.nx), ty = (dy-dot*hit.ny);
    const slideAngle = wrap(car.angle+80*Math.sign(dot*alongWall));
    if (!sweepCab(car,district,tx,ty)) {
      car.x += tx; car.y += ty; car.angle = slideAngle;
    }
  }
  return Number(headOn); // SKIPIT 0x26489 requests the impact cue on this branch.
}

export function cameraPose(car, district) {
  const angle = car.camera ? (car.chaseYaw ?? car.angle*Math.PI/2880) :
    car.angle*Math.PI/2880+car.lookYaw;
  const fx = Math.sin(angle), fy = Math.cos(angle);
  if (!car.camera) {
    return {x:architectureXY(car.x),y:architectureXY(car.y),z:vehicleZ(car.z)+car.seatOffset,fx,fy,angle,pitch:car.lookPitch};
  }
  const floorLook = car.chaseFloorLook ?? 0;
  const distance = chaseRange.near+(car.chaseDistance-chaseRange.near)*Math.exp(-2*floorLook);
  const centerZ = district.taxiCenterZ ?? (cabBody.base-cabBody.height/2);
  const bottom = (district.taxiBounds ?? fallbackBounds).bottom;
  const orbitPitch = chasePitch(car,car.chaseDistance,centerZ,bottom);
  const lift = floorLook > 0 ? centerZ-bottom+2 : distance*Math.tan(orbitPitch);
  const pitch = floorLook > 0 ? Math.max(-chasePitchLimit,Math.atan2(lift,distance)-floorLook) : orbitPitch;
  const targetZ = vehicleZ(car.z)+centerZ;
  const cabTop = vehicleZ(car.z)+(district.taxiBounds ?? fallbackBounds).top;
  const x=architectureXY(car.x), y=architectureXY(car.y);
  const clear = chaseClearance(district,x,y,targetZ,-fx*distance,-fy*distance,-lift,cabTop);
  return {x:x-fx*distance*clear,y:y-fy*distance*clear,z:targetZ-lift*clear,
    fx,fy,angle,pitch};
}

.\fli.js

// Retail 320×200 Autodesk Animator FLI files. Delta frames retain the previous index buffer.
export function* fliFrames(bytes, indices, filename) {
  const view = new DataView(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength);
  const u16 = pos => view.getUint16(pos, true);
  const u32 = pos => view.getUint32(pos, true);
  const width = 320;
  const height = 200;
  if (bytes.length < 128 || u16(4) !== 0xaf11 || u16(8) !== width || u16(10) !== height ||
      u16(12) !== 8 || u16(6) === 0) throw new Error('Unrecognized FLI header in ' + filename);
  indices.fill(0);
  const palette = new Uint8Array(256 * 3);
  // 0x4B755 waits floor(header speed * 18 / 60) ticks of the DOS timer.
  const duration = Math.floor(u16(16) * 18 / 60) * 1000 / 18.2065;
  let offset = 128;
  for (let frame = 0; frame < u16(6); frame++) {
    const end = offset + u32(offset);
    if (end < offset + 16 || end > bytes.length || u16(offset + 4) !== 0xf1fa) {
      throw new Error('Invalid FLI frame.');
    }
    let paletteChanged = frame === 0;
    let chunk = offset + 16;
    for (let n = 0; n < u16(offset + 6); n++) {
      if (chunk + 6 > end) throw new Error('Incomplete FLI chunk.');
      const limit = chunk + u32(chunk);
      if (limit < chunk + 6 || limit > end) throw new Error('Invalid FLI chunk length.');
      let pos = chunk + 6;
      function byte() {
        if (pos >= limit) throw new Error('Incomplete FLI packet.');
        return bytes[pos++];
      }
      function word() { return byte() + byte() * 256; }
      function span(y, x, count, repeat) {
        if (x + count > width || y >= height) throw new Error('FLI scanline exceeds frame.');
        const dest = y * width + x;
        if (repeat) indices.fill(byte(), dest, dest + count);
        else for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) indices[dest + i] = byte();
      }
      switch (u16(chunk + 4)) {
        case 11: { // COLOR_64
          paletteChanged = true;
          const packets = word();
          let index = 0;
          for (let i = 0; i < packets; i++) {
            index += byte();
            const count = byte() || 256;
            if (index + count > 256) throw new Error('Invalid FLI palette range.');
            for (let c = index * 3; c < (index + count) * 3; c++) {
              const value = byte() & 63;
              palette[c] = value;
            }
            index += count;
          }
          break;
        }
        case 13: // BLACK
          indices.fill(0);
          break;
        case 16: // COPY
          if (limit - pos < indices.length) throw new Error('Incomplete FLI copy chunk.');
          indices.set(bytes.subarray(pos, pos + indices.length));
          break;
        case 15: // BRUN
          for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
            byte(); // Historical packet count. Width delimits the scanline.
            let x = 0;
            while (x < width) {
              const run = byte();
              const count = run < 128 ? run : 256 - run;
              if (!count) throw new Error('Empty FLI run.');
              span(y, x, count, run < 128);
              x += count;
            }
          }
          break;
        case 12: { // FLI_LC
          const first = word();
          const lines = word();
          if (first + lines > height) throw new Error('Invalid FLI delta region.');
          for (let y = first; y < first + lines; y++) {
            const packets = byte();
            let x = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < packets; i++) {
              x += byte();
              const run = byte();
              const count = run < 128 ? run : 256 - run;
              span(y, x, count, run >= 128);
              x += count;
            }
          }
          break;
        }
        default: throw new Error('Unsupported FLI chunk ' + u16(chunk + 4));
      }
      chunk = limit;
    }
    yield { palette, duration, paletteChanged };
    offset = end;
  }
}

const images = new Map();

export function loadHDImage(name) {
  const stem = /^([A-Za-z0-9_.-]+)\.(?:img|gif)$/i.exec(name)?.[1];
  if (!stem) return Promise.resolve(null);
  if (!images.has(stem)) images.set(stem, readPNG(stem));
  return images.get(stem);
}

async function readPNG(stem) {
  for (const extension of ['png', 'PNG']) {
    const url = new URL(`./HD/${encodeURIComponent(stem)}.${extension}`, import.meta.url);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      if (response.ok) return await createImageBitmap(await response.blob());
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn(`Unable to load ${url.pathname}`, error);
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function uploadBitmap(device, bitmap, destination, width=bitmap.width, height=bitmap.height, x=0, y=0) {
  try {
    device.queue.copyExternalImageToTexture({source:bitmap,origin:[x,y]},destination,[width,height]);
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof TypeError)) throw error;
    // Some adapters cannot import a decoded image directly. Preserve its full raster.
    const canvas = new OffscreenCanvas(width,height);
    const pixels = canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
    if (!pixels) throw error;
    pixels.drawImage(bitmap,x,y,width,height,0,0,width,height);
    device.queue.writeTexture(destination,pixels.getImageData(0,0,width,height).data,
      {bytesPerRow:width*4},[width,height]);
    canvas.width = 0;
  }
}

export function createHDTexture(device, bitmap) {
  const limit = device.limits.maxTextureDimension2D;
  if (!bitmap || bitmap.width > limit || bitmap.height > limit) return null;
  const size = [bitmap.width, bitmap.height];
  const texture = device.createTexture({size, format: 'rgba8unorm',
    usage: GPUTextureUsage.TEXTURE_BINDING | GPUTextureUsage.COPY_DST | GPUTextureUsage.RENDER_ATTACHMENT});
  uploadBitmap(device,bitmap,{texture});
  return texture;
}

.\indexed-screen.js

// SKIPIT writes the same 320×200 image through four VGA map masks. Each
// 16,000-byte plane holds every fourth horizontal pixel (0x3481E, 0x34B7A).
const WIDTH = 320;
const HEIGHT = 200;
const PLANE = WIDTH * HEIGHT / 4;
const retraceMs = 1000 / 70;
export const retraces = count => new Promise(done => setTimeout(done, count * retraceMs));

function decodeIndices(data, minBits) {
  if (minBits < 2 || minBits > 8) throw new Error('Invalid image code size.');
  const clear = 1 << minBits;
  const prefix = new Uint16Array(4096);
  const suffix = new Uint8Array(4096);
  const stack = new Uint8Array(4097);
  const out = new Uint8Array(WIDTH * HEIGHT);
  let written = 0, bit = 0, bits = minBits + 1, next = clear + 2, previous = -1, first = 0;
  while (bit + bits <= data.length * 8) {
    const p = bit >>> 3;
    const word = data[p] | data[p + 1] << 8 | data[p + 2] << 16;
    const code = word >>> (bit & 7) & ((1 << bits) - 1);
    bit += bits;
    if (code === clear) {
      next = clear + 2;
      bits = minBits + 1;
      previous = -1;
      continue;
    }
    if (code === clear + 1) {
      if (written === out.length) return out;
      break;
    }
    if (code > next || (previous < 0 && code >= clear)) break;
    let size = 0;
    let link = code;
    if (code === next) {
      stack[size++] = first;
      link = previous;
    }
    while (link >= clear) {
      stack[size++] = suffix[link];
      link = prefix[link];
    }
    first = link;
    stack[size++] = first;
    if (written + size > out.length) break;
    while (size) out[written++] = stack[--size];
    if (previous >= 0 && next < 4096) {
      prefix[next] = previous;
      suffix[next++] = first;
      if (next === 1 << bits && bits < 12) bits++;
    }
    previous = code;
  }
  throw new Error('Incomplete or invalid image pixel stream.');
}

export function readScreen(bytes, filename) {
  const view = new DataView(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength);
  const fail = () => { throw new Error('Unsupported image in ' + filename); };
  const signature = new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.subarray(0, 6));
  if (bytes.length < 781 || !['IMAGEX', 'GIF87a', 'GIF89a'].includes(signature) ||
      view.getUint16(6, true) !== WIDTH || view.getUint16(8, true) !== HEIGHT ||
      (bytes[10] & 0x87) !== 0x87) fail();
  let pos = 13;
  function palette(count) {
    if (pos + 3 * count > bytes.length) fail();
    const dac = new Uint8Array(768);
    for (let i = 0; i < count * 3; i++) dac[i] = bytes[pos++] >>> 2;
    return dac;
  }
  function blocks(collect) {
    const start = pos;
    const packed = collect && new Uint8Array(bytes.length - start);
    let used = 0;
    while (pos < bytes.length) {
      const size = bytes[pos++];
      if (!size) return collect ? packed.subarray(0, used) : null;
      if (pos + size > bytes.length) fail();
      if (collect) packed.set(bytes.subarray(pos, pos + size), used);
      used += size;
      pos += size;
    }
    fail();
  }
  let dac = palette(256);
  while (bytes[pos] === 0x21) { pos += 2; blocks(false); }
  if (bytes[pos++] !== 0x2c || pos + 10 > bytes.length ||
      view.getUint32(pos, true) !== 0 || view.getUint16(pos + 4, true) !== WIDTH ||
      view.getUint16(pos + 6, true) !== HEIGHT) fail();
  const flags = bytes[pos + 8];
  pos += 9;
  if (flags & 0x40) fail();
  if (flags & 0x80) dac = palette(2 << (flags & 7));
  const minBits = bytes[pos++];
  const indices = decodeIndices(blocks(true), minBits);
  const planes = new Uint8Array(indices.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < indices.length; i++) planes[(i & 3) * PLANE + (i >>> 2)] = indices[i];
  return { planes, dac };
}

export function readFont(bytes) {
  if (bytes[0] !== 59 || bytes.length !== 3367) throw new Error('Unrecognized COPYFONT.SPR.');
  return readSpriteBank(bytes, 59);
}

export function readSpriteBank(bytes, count, {planar = true} = {}) {
  if (bytes[0] !== count || bytes.length < 1 + 2 * count) throw new Error('Unsupported DOS sprite bank.');
  let pos = 1 + 2 * count;
  return Array.from({length: count}, (_, i) => {
    const width = bytes[1 + 2 * i], height = bytes[2 + 2 * i];
    const end = pos + width * height;
    if (!width || (planar && width % 4) || !height || end > bytes.length) throw new Error('Invalid sprite descriptor.');
    const pixels = bytes.subarray(pos, end);
    pos = end;
    return {width, height, pixels};
  });
}

// Opaque blit at 0x234BC and its index-zero-transparent variant at 0x232CC.
export function drawSprite(planes, {pixels, width, height}, x, y, transparent = false, coverage) {
  if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x + width > WIDTH || y + height > HEIGHT) return;
  const stride = width / 4;
  let src = 0;
  for (let mask = 0; mask < 4; mask++) {
    const column = x + mask;
    let dest = (column & 3) * PLANE + y * 80 + (column >>> 2);
    for (let row = 0; row < height; row++, dest += 80) {
      for (let i = 0; i < stride; i++) {
        const index = pixels[src++];
        if (!transparent || index) {
          planes[dest + i] = index;
          if (coverage) coverage[dest + i] = 1;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

export function drawLine(planes, text, y, widths, glyphs, left, bank = 0, coverage) {
  const chars = Array.from(text, c => c.charCodeAt(0) - 32);
  let x = left ?? Math.trunc((WIDTH - chars.reduce((sum, i) => sum + widths[i], 0)) / 2);
  for (const i of chars) {
    drawSprite(planes, glyphs[bank + i], x, y, false, coverage);
    x += widths[i];
    if (x > 300) break;
  }
}

export function indexedDisplay(display, step) {
  if (step < 1 || step > 63) throw new Error('Invalid SKIPIT.EXE palette step.');
  const planes = new Uint8Array(4 * PLANE);
  const dac = new Uint8Array(768);
  const coverage = new Uint8Array(planes.length);
  let bitmap, brightness = 0;
  function setImage(image) {
    planes.set(image.planes);
    bitmap = image.hd;
    if (bitmap) coverage.fill(0);
  }
  function sprite(image, x, y, transparent) {
    drawSprite(planes, image, x, y, transparent, bitmap ? coverage : undefined);
  }
  function line(text, y, widths, glyphs, left, bank) {
    drawLine(planes, text, y, widths, glyphs, left, bank, bitmap ? coverage : undefined);
  }
  function present(pixelsChanged = true) {
    display.presentIndexed(planes, dac, pixelsChanged, false, true,
      bitmap && {bitmap, brightness, coverage});
  }
  async function fade(target, inward, movieFrame) {
    const next = new Uint8Array(768);
    if (!inward) next.set(target);
    let uploadPlanes = true;
    const hd = bitmap && !movieFrame;
    const peak = hd ? Math.max(...target) || 63 : 0;
    let level = inward ? 0 : peak;
    while (true) {
      let changed = false;
      for (let i = 0; i < next.length; i++) {
        const remaining = inward ? target[i] - next[i] : next[i];
        if (!remaining) continue;
        const delta = remaining >= step ? step : 1;
        next[i] += inward ? delta : -delta;
        changed = true;
      }
      if (hd) {
        const remaining = inward ? peak - level : level;
        if (remaining) {
          const delta = remaining >= step ? step : 1;
          level += inward ? delta : -delta;
          brightness = level / peak;
          changed = true;
        }
      }
      if (!changed) return;
      // Both DAC loops pause at the midpoint of the RGB component stream.
      for (const first of [0, 384]) {
        await retraces(1);
        dac.set(next.subarray(first, first + 384), first);
        if (movieFrame) display.presentIndexed(movieFrame, dac, uploadPlanes, true);
        else present(uploadPlanes);
        uploadPlanes = false;
      }
    }
  }
  return { setImage, sprite, line, present, fade };
}

.\intro.js
────────────────────

// 0x49FD0 reads FONT.SPR as row-major byte masks. Each '#' brightens the
// underlying movie pixel. The widths in this bank also give text advances.
export function readMaskFont(bytes) {
  const count = bytes[0];
  let offset = 1 + count * 2;
  if (count !== 96 || offset > bytes.length) throw new Error('Invalid FONT.SPR directory.');
  return Array.from({length: count}, (_, i) => {
    const width = bytes[1 + i * 2], height = bytes[2 + i * 2];
    const end = offset + width * height;
    if (end > bytes.length) throw new Error('Incomplete FONT.SPR glyph.');
    const mask = bytes.subarray(offset, end);
    offset = end;
    return {width, height, mask};
  });
}

function buildHighlights(palette) {
  // 0x4A29B builds four brightening maps in six-bit DAC space. Search
  // exhaustively here; equal distances use the first palette entry.
  return [40, 29, 18, 7].map(boost => Uint8Array.from({length: 256}, (_, index) => {
    const r = Math.min(63, palette[index * 3] + boost);
    const g = Math.min(63, palette[index * 3 + 1] + boost);
    const b = Math.min(63, palette[index * 3 + 2] + boost);
    let closest = 0, best = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
      const distance = (palette[i * 3] - r) ** 2 + (palette[i * 3 + 1] - g) ** 2 + (palette[i * 3 + 2] - b) ** 2;
      if (distance < best) { best = distance; closest = i; }
    }
    return closest;
  }));
}

export function storyCrawl(linear, palette, fontBytes, lines) {
  const background = linear.slice();
  const font = readMaskFont(fontBytes);
  const rows = lines.map(line => {
    const glyphs = Array.from(line, c => font[c.charCodeAt(0) - 32]).filter(Boolean);
    return {glyphs, left: 160 - Math.floor(glyphs.reduce((sum, g) => sum + g.width, 0) / 2)};
  });
  const maps = buildHighlights(palette);

  // 0x4A104 advances one source line every eight scroll steps. Draw up to
  // thirteen lines with the original four brightness bands at the margins.
  return function draw(step) {
    linear.set(background);
    const first = Math.floor(step / 8);
    for (let i = 0; i < 13 && first + i < rows.length; i++) {
      const {glyphs, left} = rows[first + i];
      const top = 10 - (step % 8) + i * 8;
      let x = left;
      for (const {width, height, mask} of glyphs) {
        for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
          const row = top + y;
          if (row < 6 || row > 114) continue;
          const edge = Math.min(row - 6, 114 - row);
          const map = maps[Math.max(0, 3 - Math.floor(edge / 4))];
          const start = row * 320 + x;
          for (let dx = 0; dx < width; dx++) {
            if (mask[y * width + dx] === 35 && x + dx >= 0 && x + dx < 320)
              linear[start + dx] = map[linear[start + dx]];
          }
        }
        x += width;
      }
    }
    return first + 13 > rows.length;
  };
}

const manualVerifiedKey = 'qwebgpu.manualVerified';
