// Shares the district camera, point lights and night cubemap with the street pass.
export const taxiShader = `
struct TaxiMaterial { base: vec4f, surface: vec4f, lamp: vec4f };
@group(1) @binding(0) var<uniform> finish: TaxiMaterial;
@group(1) @binding(1) var paintSampler: sampler;
@group(1) @binding(2) var baseMap: texture_2d<f32>;
@group(1) @binding(3) var metalRoughMap: texture_2d<f32>;
@group(1) @binding(4) var normalMap: texture_2d<f32>;
struct TaxiVertex {
  @builtin(position) pos: vec4f, @location(0) uv: vec2f, @location(1) world: vec3f,
  @location(2) normal: vec3f, @location(3) tangent: vec4f,
  @location(4) metalUV: vec2f, @location(5) normalUV: vec2f
};
fn cabDirection(local:vec3f) -> vec3f {
  let forward = view.outputSize.zw;
  return vec3f(local.x*vec2f(-forward.y,forward.x)+local.y*forward,local.z);
}
@vertex fn taxiVertex(@location(0) point:vec3f, @location(1) normal:vec3f,
                      @location(2) uv:vec2f, @location(3) tangent:vec4f,
                      @location(4) metalUV:vec2f, @location(5) normalUV:vec2f) -> TaxiVertex {
  let offset = cabDirection(point);
  let d = (view.player.xyz-view.eye.xyz)+offset-cabDirection(view.cabSeat.xyz);
  let along = dot(d.xy,view.axes.xy);
  let depth = along*view.axes.z+d.z*view.axes.w;
  let screen = vec2f(dot(d.xy,vec2f(-view.axes.y,view.axes.x)),along*view.axes.w-d.z*view.axes.z);
  let projection = select(1.0,depth,view.cabSeat.w != 0.0);
  return TaxiVertex(vec4f(screen*view.scale.xy*projection,view.backdrop.x,depth),uv,view.player.xyz+offset,
                    cabDirection(normal),vec4f(cabDirection(tangent.xyz),tangent.w),metalUV,normalUV);
}

fn cabLitColor(baseColor:vec3f, rough:f32, metal:f32, worldPos:vec3f, n:vec3f, eye:vec3f) -> vec3f {
  let nv = clamp(dot(n,eye),0.001,1.0);
  let f0 = mix(vec3f(0.04),baseColor,metal);
  let diffuse = baseColor*(1.0-metal);
  let reflected = reflect(-eye,n);
  let skyDir = vec3f(reflected.x,-reflected.z,reflected.y);
  let skyLight = textureSampleLevel(stars,skySampler,skyDir,0.0).r;
  let rim = pow(1.0-nv,5.0);
  let envFresnel = f0+(max(vec3f(1.0-rough),f0)-f0)*rim;
  let sky = view.ambient.rgb;
  let skyFacing = smoothstep(-0.25,0.75,skyDir.y);
  let neutralSky = vec3f(dot(sky,vec3f(0.2126,0.7152,0.0722)));
  let reflectionSky = mix(sky,mix(neutralSky,sky,0.25)*0.7,metal);
  let skyReflection = reflectionSky*(0.05+0.70*skyFacing+skyLight*0.35*(1.0-rough));
  var reflection = skyReflection;
  if (view.textureEffects.z > 0.0) {
    let worldReflection = pow(textureSampleLevel(reflections,skySampler,skyDir,0.0).rgb,vec3f(2.2));
    reflection = mix(skyReflection,worldReflection,metal);
  }
  var linear = diffuse*sky*(0.065+0.16*clamp(-n.z,0.0,1.0))+reflection*envFresnel;

  // Evaluate each nearby fixture in world space. Paint, glass and chrome
  // share the light position and color but retain their authored roughness.
  let range = lampRange(worldPos);
  for (var i=range.x; i<range.y; i++) {
    let lamp = lights[lightBins[i]];
    if (behindEmitter(lamp,worldPos)) { continue; }
    let delta = lamp.positionRadius.xyz-worldPos;
    let d2 = dot(delta,delta);
    let falloff = 1.0-d2*lamp.positionRadius.w;
    if (falloff <= 0.0) { continue; }
    let l = delta*inverseSqrt(max(d2,0.0001));
    let nl = dot(n,l);
    let radiance = lamp.rgb.xyz*(2.0*falloff*falloff);
    if (lamp.rgb.w < 0.0) { linear += radiance*diffuse*0.2; }
    if (nl <= 0.0) { continue; }
    let halfway = l+eye;
    let h = halfway*inverseSqrt(max(dot(halfway,halfway),0.0001));
    let nh = max(0.0,dot(n,h));
    let lightRoughness = max(rough,abs(lamp.rgb.w));
    let a2 = pow(lightRoughness,4.0);
    let k = (lightRoughness+1.0)*(lightRoughness+1.0)/8.0;
    let d = nh*nh*(a2-1.0)+1.0;
    let distribution = a2/max(3.14159*d*d,0.0000001);
    let visibility = 0.25/((nv*(1.0-k)+k)*(nl*(1.0-k)+k));
    let fresnel = f0+(1.0-f0)*pow(1.0-clamp(dot(eye,h),0.0,1.0),5.0);
    let incident = radiance*nl;
    linear += incident*(diffuse*(1.0-fresnel)+fresnel*distribution*visibility);
  }
  return pow(max(linear,vec3f(0.0)),vec3f(1.0/2.2))*view.settings.x;
}

fn taxiTexel(p:vec2i,fallback:vec3f) -> vec3f {
  let size = vec2i(textureDimensions(baseMap));
  let wrapped = ((p % size)+size) % size;
  let sample = textureLoad(baseMap,wrapped,0);
  return select(fallback,sample.rgb,sample.a > 0.0);
}
@fragment fn taxiPixel(p:TaxiVertex) -> @location(0) vec4f {
  let dx = dpdx(p.uv);
  let dy = dpdy(p.uv);
  let original = textureSample(baseMap,paintSampler,p.uv);
  let mr = textureSample(metalRoughMap,paintSampler,p.metalUV);
  let mapped = textureSample(normalMap,paintSampler,p.normalUV).xyz*2.0-1.0;
  var baseColor = original.rgb;
  let size = vec2f(textureDimensions(baseMap));
  let upscale = view.textureEffects.x;
  let sharpness = view.textureEffects.y;
  if (upscale > 1.0 && max(length(dx*size),length(dy*size)) <= 1.0) {
    let texel = p.uv*size;
    let at = vec2i(floor(texel));
    let f = (floor(fract(texel)*upscale)+0.5)/upscale;
    let side = vec2i(select(-1,1,f.x>=0.5),select(-1,1,f.y>=0.5));
    let center = taxiTexel(at,original.rgb);
    let c = array<vec3f,12>(center,
      taxiTexel(at+vec2i(side.x,0),center),taxiTexel(at+vec2i(0,side.y),center),
      taxiTexel(at+side,center),taxiTexel(at+vec2i(side.x,-side.y),center),
      taxiTexel(at+vec2i(-side.x,side.y),center),taxiTexel(at+vec2i(2*side.x,0),center),
      taxiTexel(at+vec2i(0,2*side.y),center),taxiTexel(at+vec2i(2*side.x,side.y),center),
      taxiTexel(at+vec2i(side.x,2*side.y),center),taxiTexel(at+vec2i(-side.x,0),center),
      taxiTexel(at+vec2i(0,-side.y),center));
    baseColor = textureEdge(c,f,side);
  }
  if (sharpness > 0.0) {
    let step = max(1.0/size,max(abs(dx),abs(dy)));
    let n = textureSampleGrad(baseMap,paintSampler,p.uv+vec2f(0,-step.y),dx,dy).rgb;
    let s = textureSampleGrad(baseMap,paintSampler,p.uv+vec2f(0,step.y),dx,dy).rgb;
    let w = textureSampleGrad(baseMap,paintSampler,p.uv+vec2f(-step.x,0),dx,dy).rgb;
    let e = textureSampleGrad(baseMap,paintSampler,p.uv+vec2f(step.x,0),dx,dy).rgb;
    baseColor = clamp(baseColor+textureSharpen(original.rgb,n,s,w,e,sharpness)-original.rgb,vec3f(0),vec3f(1));
  }
  if (finish.lamp.w > 1.5) {
    if (dot(p.normal,view.eye.xyz+cabDirection(view.cabSeat.xyz)-p.world) <= 0.0) {
      return vec4f(vec3f(0.012,0.012,0.014)*view.settings.x,1.0);
    }
    let dimensions = vec2i(textureDimensions(wayfinder));
    let pixel = clamp(vec2i(p.uv*vec2f(dimensions)),vec2i(0),dimensions-1);
    let display = textureLoad(wayfinder,pixel,0);
    var halo = vec3f(0.0);
    let offsets = array<vec2i,4>(vec2i(-1,0),vec2i(1,0),vec2i(0,-1),vec2i(0,1));
    for (var i=0u; i<4u; i++) {
      let neighbor = textureLoad(wayfinder,clamp(pixel+offsets[i],vec2i(0),dimensions-1),0);
      halo += neighbor.rgb*neighbor.a;
    }
    let time = view.skyTint.w;
    let scanline = 0.86+0.14*cos(p.uv.y*f32(dimensions.y)*6.2831853);
    let sweep = exp(-pow((fract(p.uv.y-time*0.18)-0.5)*15.0,2.0));
    let pulse = 0.90+0.10*sin(time*2.7);
    let edge = 1.0-0.15*dot(p.uv*2.0-1.0,p.uv*2.0-1.0);
    let glow = mix(vec3f(0.012,0.004,0.006),display.rgb,display.a)+halo*0.08;
    return vec4f((glow*(scanline*pulse+0.35*sweep)+vec3f(0.04,0.009,0.006)*sweep)*
      edge*view.settings.x,1.0);
  }
  let base = vec4f(baseColor,original.a)*finish.base;
  let rough = select(clamp(mr.g*finish.surface.y,0.045,1.0),0.10,finish.lamp.z > 0.0);
  let metal = select(clamp(mr.b*finish.surface.x,0.0,1.0),1.0,finish.lamp.z > 0.0);
  let eye = normalize(view.eye.xyz+cabDirection(view.cabSeat.xyz)-p.world);
  var n = normalize(p.normal);
  if (abs(p.tangent.w)>0.5) {
    let t = normalize(p.tangent.xyz-n*dot(p.tangent.xyz,n));
    let b = cross(n,t)*sign(p.tangent.w);
    n = normalize(n*mapped.z+(t*mapped.x+b*mapped.y)*finish.surface.z);
  }
  if (dot(n,eye)<0.0) { n = -n; }
  let rim = pow(1.0-clamp(dot(n,eye),0.0,1.0),5.0);
  let rgb = cabLitColor(base.rgb,rough,metal,p.world,n,eye);
  // These thin glass primitives have no authored volume. Composite their tint
  // and grazing reflection over the street, seats and other lamps behind them.
  let alpha = mix(base.a,base.a*(0.42+0.58*rim),finish.surface.w);
  if (finish.lamp.x > 0.0 && view.headlamps[0].w > 0.0) {
    let front = smoothstep(0.0,0.45,dot(eye.xy,view.outputSize.zw));
    return vec4f(mix(rgb,vec3f(view.settings.x),front),mix(alpha,1.0,front));
  }
  if (finish.lamp.y > 0.0 && dot(p.world.xy-view.player.xy,view.outputSize.zw) < -20.0) {
    let rear = smoothstep(0.0,0.45,-dot(eye.xy,view.outputSize.zw));
    let red = mix(vec3f(0.68,0.012,0.006),vec3f(1.0,0.12,0.04),view.taillamps[0].w);
    return vec4f(mix(rgb,red*view.settings.x,rear),mix(alpha,1.0,rear));
  }
  return vec4f(rgb,alpha);
}
@fragment fn taxiWelcome(@builtin(position) pos:vec4f) -> @location(0) vec4f {
  let scale = min(view.outputSize.x/320.0,view.outputSize.y/200.0);
  let p = vec2i(floor((pos.xy-view.outputSize.xy*0.5)/scale+vec2f(160,100)));
  if (any(p<vec2i(0)) || any(p>=vec2i(320,200))) { discard; }
  let ink = textureLoad(cockpit,p+vec2i(0,i32(view.settings.w)),0).r;
  if (ink == 0u) { discard; }
  return color(ink);
}`;

export async function prepareTaxiPipelines(device,format,depthFormat,module,sceneLayout) {
  const materialLayout = device.createBindGroupLayout({entries:[
    {binding:0,visibility:GPUShaderStage.FRAGMENT,buffer:{type:'uniform'}},
    {binding:1,visibility:GPUShaderStage.FRAGMENT,sampler:{type:'filtering'}},
    ...[2,3,4].map(binding => ({binding,visibility:GPUShaderStage.FRAGMENT,texture:{sampleType:'float'}}))]});
  const layout = device.createPipelineLayout({bindGroupLayouts:[sceneLayout,materialLayout]});
  const buffers = [{arrayStride:64,attributes:[
    {shaderLocation:0,offset:0,format:'float32x3'},{shaderLocation:1,offset:12,format:'float32x3'},
    {shaderLocation:2,offset:24,format:'float32x2'},{shaderLocation:3,offset:32,format:'float32x4'},
    {shaderLocation:4,offset:48,format:'float32x2'},{shaderLocation:5,offset:56,format:'float32x2'}]}];
  const [opaque,glass] = await Promise.all([false,true].map(transparent => device.createRenderPipelineAsync({
    layout,vertex:{module,entryPoint:'taxiVertex',buffers},
    fragment:{module,entryPoint:'taxiPixel',targets:[{format,blend:transparent ? {
      color:{srcFactor:'src-alpha',dstFactor:'one-minus-src-alpha'},
      alpha:{srcFactor:'one',dstFactor:'one-minus-src-alpha'}} : undefined}]},
    primitive:{topology:'triangle-list',cullMode:'none'},
    depthStencil:{format:depthFormat,depthWriteEnabled:!transparent,depthCompare:'greater-equal'},
  })));
  return {materialLayout,opaque,glass};
}


────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

.\taxi-model.js
─────────────────────────

import {Box3, Matrix3, Matrix4, Vector3} from './model-math.js';
import {uploadBitmap} from './hd-images.js';
import {cabBody,rideHeight} from './cab-dimensions.js';

// Model-space metres in the supplied GLB. The wheel is at (+0.42, 1.09, +0.80)
// and the headlights face +Z. Place the eye above and behind the wheel.
const driverEye = [0.42,1.42,0.34];
const assetURL = new URL('./HD/cars/taxi/taxi.glb',import.meta.url);
const monitorURL = new URL('./HD/cars/taxi/commodore_64__computer_full_pack.glb',import.meta.url);
const cache = new WeakMap();
const wheelNodes = new Set(['Cylinder.004_2','Cylinder.005_3','Cylinder.006_4','Cylinder.007_5',
  'Cylinder.008_6','Cylinder.009_7','Cylinder.010_8','Cylinder.011_9']);

function readGeometry(gltf, binary, monitor, cabRatio) {
  function accessor(id, components, componentType) {
    const a = gltf.accessors[id], v = gltf.bufferViews[a.bufferView];
    if (a.componentType !== componentType || (v.byteStride && v.byteStride !== components*4)) {
      throw new Error('Unexpected vertex layout in vehicle model');
    }
    const ArrayType = componentType === 5126 ? Float32Array : Uint32Array;
    return new ArrayType(binary.buffer,binary.byteOffset+(v.byteOffset ?? 0)+(a.byteOffset ?? 0),a.count*components);
  }
  const pieces = [];
  let vertexCount = 0, indexCount = 0;
  function visit(id,parent,selected = !monitor,hidden = false) {
    const node = gltf.nodes[id];
    const matrix = node.matrix ? parent.clone().multiply(new Matrix4().fromArray(node.matrix)) : parent;
    selected ||= node.name === 'video monitor 1702_6';
    hidden ||= !monitor && wheelNodes.has(node.name);
    if (selected && node.mesh !== undefined) {
      for (const primitive of gltf.meshes[node.mesh].primitives) {
        const {POSITION,NORMAL,TANGENT,TEXCOORD_0} = primitive.attributes;
        const position = accessor(POSITION,3,5126), indices = accessor(primitive.indices,1,5125);
        const material = gltf.materials[primitive.material];
        function textureUV(info) {
          const id = primitive.attributes['TEXCOORD_'+(info?.texCoord ?? 0)];
          if (id === undefined) throw new Error('Missing texture coordinates in vehicle model');
          return accessor(id,2,5126);
        }
        const plateUV = !monitor && material.name === 'numberplate' ? gltf.accessors[TEXCOORD_0] : null;
        const boundsOnly = hidden || (monitor && material.name === 'monitor_screen');
        pieces.push({matrix,position,indices,normal:accessor(NORMAL,3,5126),uv:accessor(TEXCOORD_0,2,5126),plateUV,
          metalUV:textureUV(material.pbrMetallicRoughness.metallicRoughnessTexture),
          normalUV:textureUV(material.normalTexture),
          tangent:TANGENT === undefined ? null : accessor(TANGENT,4,5126),material:primitive.material,boundsOnly});
        if (!boundsOnly) {
          vertexCount += position.length/3;
          indexCount += indices.length;
        }
      }
    }
    for (const child of node.children ?? []) visit(child,matrix,selected,hidden);
  }
  for (const root of gltf.scenes[gltf.scene].nodes) visit(root,new Matrix4());
  if (!pieces.length) throw new Error('No matching geometry in vehicle model');
  // Pack opaque indices in material draw order. Keep the source order of glass
  // pieces so depth ties retain their original blending order.
  const order = piece => gltf.materials[piece.material].alphaMode === 'BLEND' ? gltf.materials.length : piece.material;
  pieces.sort((a,b) => order(a)-order(b));
  const vertices = new Float32Array((vertexCount+(monitor ? 4 : 0))*16);
  const indices = new Uint32Array(indexCount+(monitor ? 6 : 0));
  const low = new Vector3(Infinity,Infinity,Infinity), high = new Vector3(-Infinity,-Infinity,-Infinity);
  const p = new Vector3(), n = new Vector3(), t = new Vector3();
  const draws = [], lensCenters = [], rearBulbs = Array.from({length:6},() => new Box3()), screenBounds = new Box3();
  let first = 0, base = 0;
  for (const piece of pieces) {
    const {matrix,position,normal,tangent,uv,metalUV,normalUV} = piece;
    const normalMatrix = new Matrix3().getNormalMatrix(matrix), mirrored = matrix.determinant() < 0;
    const center = new Vector3();
    const lens = gltf.materials[piece.material].name === 'headlight_glass' ? new Box3() : null;
    const tailGlass = !monitor && gltf.materials[piece.material].name === 'tail_light';
    for (let i=0; i<position.length/3; i++) {
      p.fromArray(position,3*i).applyMatrix4(matrix);
      low.min(p); high.max(p); center.add(p);
      if (monitor && gltf.materials[piece.material].name === 'monitor_screen') screenBounds.expandByPoint(p);
      lens?.expandByPoint(p);
      // The rear face carries three separate lens islands on each side.
      if (tailGlass && p.z < -2.55) {
        const row = p.y < 0.87 ? 0 : p.y < 0.985 ? 1 : 2;
        rearBulbs[3*Number(p.x >= 0)+row].expandByPoint(p);
      }
      if (piece.boundsOnly) continue;
      n.fromArray(normal,3*i).applyNormalMatrix(normalMatrix);
      if (tangent) t.fromArray(tangent,4*i).transformDirection(matrix);
      else t.set(0,0,0);
      const at = (base+i)*16;
      let u = uv[i*2], v = uv[i*2+1];
      if (piece.plateUV) {
        // The original plate occupies one rectangle in a lettering atlas.
        const {min,max} = piece.plateUV;
        u = (u-min[0])/(max[0]-min[0]);
        v = (max[1]-v)/(max[1]-min[1]);
      }
      vertices.set([p.x,p.y,p.z,-n.x,n.z,-n.y,u,v,-t.x,t.z,-t.y,
        tangent ? tangent[i*4+3]*(mirrored ? -1 : 1) : 0,
        metalUV[i*2],metalUV[i*2+1],normalUV[i*2],normalUV[i*2+1]],at);
    }
    if (piece.boundsOnly) continue;
    for (let i=0; i<piece.indices.length; i+=3) {
      indices[first+i] = base+piece.indices[i];
      indices[first+i+1] = base+piece.indices[i+(mirrored ? 2 : 1)];
      indices[first+i+2] = base+piece.indices[i+(mirrored ? 1 : 2)];
    }
    if (lens) {
      const front = lens.getCenter(new Vector3());
      front.z = lens.max.z;
      lensCenters.push(front);
    }
    draws.push({first,count:piece.indices.length,material:piece.material,center:center.multiplyScalar(3/position.length)});
    first += piece.indices.length;
    base += position.length/3;
  }
  if (monitor) {
    if (screenBounds.isEmpty()) throw new Error('Missing monitor screen in vehicle model');
    const scale = 6.7/(high.x-low.x), center = screenBounds.getCenter(new Vector3());
    const mount = [5.5,12,4], depthScale = 0.3;
    const c = Math.cos(10*Math.PI/180), s = Math.sin(10*Math.PI/180);
    function direction(x,y,z) {
      return [c*x-s*z,-s*x-c*z,-y];
    }
    function fit(x,y,z) {
      x = (x-center.x)*scale; y = (y-center.y)*scale; z = (z-center.z)*scale;
      const local = direction(x,y,z*depthScale).map((value,i) => value+mount[i]);
      return [local[0]*cabRatio,local[1]*cabRatio,rideHeight+(local[2]-rideHeight)*cabRatio];
    }
    for (let at=0; at<vertices.length; at+=16) {
      vertices.set(fit(vertices[at],vertices[at+1],vertices[at+2]),at);
      vertices.set(direction(-vertices[at+3],-vertices[at+5],vertices[at+4]/depthScale),at+3);
      vertices.set(direction(-vertices[at+8],-vertices[at+10],vertices[at+9]*depthScale),at+8);
    }
    for (const draw of draws) draw.center = fit(draw.center.x,draw.center.y,draw.center.z);
    const material = gltf.materials.findIndex(m => m.name === 'monitor_screen');
    const normal = direction(0,0,1), tangent = direction(1,0,0);
    const z = screenBounds.max.z+0.01;
    const corners = [
      [screenBounds.min.x,screenBounds.max.y,0,0], [screenBounds.max.x,screenBounds.max.y,1,0],
      [screenBounds.max.x,screenBounds.min.y,1,1], [screenBounds.min.x,screenBounds.min.y,0,1],
    ];
    corners.forEach(([x,y,u,v],i) => vertices.set([
      ...fit(x,y,z),...normal,u,v,...tangent,1,u,v,u,v,
    ],(vertexCount+i)*16));
    indices.set([vertexCount,vertexCount+1,vertexCount+2,vertexCount,vertexCount+2,vertexCount+3],indexCount);
    draws.push({first:indexCount,count:6,material,center:fit(center.x,center.y,z)});
    const caseCenter = fit((low.x+high.x)/2,(low.y+high.y)/2,z);
    const caseSize = [(high.x-low.x)*scale*cabRatio,(high.y-low.y)*scale*cabRatio];
    return {vertices,indices,draws,caseCenter,caseSize,screenNormal:normal};
  }
  // Retain the accepted exterior size. Architecture has its own presentation
  // scale; fitting the chosen model eye to DOS eye height made the car too small.
  const scale = cabBody.length/(high.z-low.z), midX = (low.x+high.x)/2, midZ = (low.z+high.z)/2;
  function fit(x,y,z) { return [(midX-x)*scale,(z-midZ)*scale,rideHeight-(y-low.y)*scale]; }
  for (let at=0; at<vertices.length; at+=16) vertices.set(fit(vertices[at],vertices[at+1],vertices[at+2]),at);
  for (const draw of draws) draw.center = fit(draw.center.x,draw.center.y,draw.center.z);
  const centerZ = rideHeight-(high.y-low.y)*scale/2;
  const headlamps = lensCenters.map(p => fit(p.x,p.y,p.z));
  const taillamps = rearBulbs.filter(box => !box.isEmpty()).map(box => {
    const p = box.getCenter(new Vector3());
    p.z = box.min.z;
    return fit(p.x,p.y,p.z);
  });
  const bounds = {
    left:(midX-high.x)*scale, right:(midX-low.x)*scale,
    back:(low.z-midZ)*scale, front:(high.z-midZ)*scale,
    top:rideHeight-(high.y-low.y)*scale, bottom:rideHeight,
  };
  return {vertices,indices,draws,cabRatio:scale*(driverEye[1]-low.y)/rideHeight,eye:fit(...driverEye),centerZ,headlamps,taillamps,bounds};
}

const mipShader = `
@group(0) @binding(0) var src: texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var linearSampler: sampler;
struct Pixel { @builtin(position) pos: vec4f, @location(0) uv: vec2f };
@vertex fn vertex(@builtin(vertex_index) i:u32) -> Pixel {
  let uv = vec2f(f32((i<<1u)&2u),f32(i&2u));
  return Pixel(vec4f(uv*vec2f(2,-2)+vec2f(-1,1),0,1),uv);
}
@fragment fn fragment(p:Pixel) -> @location(0) vec4f {
  return textureSampleLevel(src,linearSampler,p.uv,0.0);
}`;

async function uploadModel(device,materialLayout,url,monitor = false,cabRatio = 1) {
  const response = await fetch(url);
  if (response.status === 404) {
    if (monitor) console.warn('Commodore dashboard model not found:',url.href);
    return null;
  }
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Unable to load '+url.pathname+' (HTTP '+response.status+')');
  const file = await response.arrayBuffer(), header = new DataView(file);
  const jsonLength = header.getUint32(12,true);
  if (header.getUint32(0,true) !== 0x46546c67 || header.getUint32(4,true) !== 2 ||
      header.getUint32(16,true) !== 0x4e4f534a || header.getUint32(24+jsonLength,true) !== 0x004e4942) {
    throw new Error('Expected a binary glTF 2.0 vehicle model');
  }
  const gltf = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(new Uint8Array(file,20,jsonLength)));
  const binary = new Uint8Array(file,28+jsonLength);
  const geometry = readGeometry(gltf,binary,monitor,cabRatio);
  const {vertices,indices,draws,centerZ,headlamps,taillamps,bounds} = geometry;
  const allocated = [];
  function buffer(data,usage) {
    const b = device.createBuffer({size:data.byteLength,usage:usage|GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST});
    allocated.push(b);
    device.queue.writeBuffer(b,0,data);
    return b;
  }
  function texture(descriptor) {
    const tex = device.createTexture(descriptor);
    allocated.push(tex);
    return tex;
  }
  try {
    const vertexBuffer = buffer(vertices,GPUBufferUsage.VERTEX), indexBuffer = buffer(indices,GPUBufferUsage.INDEX);
    const used = new Set(draws.map(draw => draw.material));
    const selectedMaterials = gltf.materials.filter((_,i) => used.has(i));
    const srgb = new Set(selectedMaterials.flatMap(m => {
      const info = m.pbrMetallicRoughness.baseColorTexture;
      return info ? [gltf.textures[info.index].source] : [];
    }));
    const neededImages = new Set(selectedMaterials.flatMap(m =>
      [m.pbrMetallicRoughness.baseColorTexture,m.pbrMetallicRoughness.metallicRoughnessTexture,m.normalTexture]
        .filter(Boolean).map(info => gltf.textures[info.index].source)));
    const module = device.createShaderModule({code:mipShader});
    const sampler = device.createSampler({minFilter:'linear',magFilter:'linear',mipmapFilter:'linear',
      maxAnisotropy:16,addressModeU:'repeat',addressModeV:'repeat'});
    const mipSampler = device.createSampler({minFilter:'linear',magFilter:'linear'});
    const pipelines = new Map();
    for (const format of ['rgba8unorm','rgba8unorm-srgb']) {
      pipelines.set(format,await device.createRenderPipelineAsync({layout:'auto',vertex:{module,entryPoint:'vertex'},
        fragment:{module,entryPoint:'fragment',targets:[{format}]}}));
    }
    const images = [];
    const plate = !monitor && selectedMaterials.find(m => m.name === 'numberplate');
    const plateSource = plate && gltf.textures[plate.pbrMetallicRoughness.baseColorTexture.index].source;
    for (const [i,image] of gltf.images.entries()) {
      if (!neededImages.has(i)) continue;
      const v = gltf.bufferViews[image.bufferView], offset = v.byteOffset ?? 0;
      let blob;
      if (i === plateSource) {
        const plateResponse = await fetch(new URL('license_plate.jpg',url));
        if (!plateResponse.ok) throw new Error('Unable to load taxi license plate (HTTP '+plateResponse.status+')');
        blob = await plateResponse.blob();
      } else {
        blob = new Blob([binary.subarray(offset,offset+v.byteLength)],{type:image.mimeType});
      }
      const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(blob,{colorSpaceConversion:'none',premultiplyAlpha:'none'});
      try {
        const format = srgb.has(i) ? 'rgba8unorm-srgb' : 'rgba8unorm';
        const levels = 1+Math.floor(Math.log2(Math.max(bitmap.width,bitmap.height)));
        const tex = texture({label:v.name,size:[bitmap.width,bitmap.height],format,mipLevelCount:levels,
          usage:GPUTextureUsage.TEXTURE_BINDING|GPUTextureUsage.COPY_DST|GPUTextureUsage.RENDER_ATTACHMENT});
        uploadBitmap(device,bitmap,{texture:tex});
        const pipeline = pipelines.get(format), encoder = device.createCommandEncoder();
        let previous = tex.createView({baseMipLevel:0,mipLevelCount:1});
        for (let level=1; level<levels; level++) {
          const next = tex.createView({baseMipLevel:level,mipLevelCount:1});
          const group = device.createBindGroup({layout:pipeline.getBindGroupLayout(0),entries:[
            {binding:0,resource:previous},{binding:1,resource:mipSampler}]});
          const pass = encoder.beginRenderPass({colorAttachments:[{view:next,loadOp:'clear',storeOp:'store',
            clearValue:[0,0,0,0]}]});
          pass.setPipeline(pipeline); pass.setBindGroup(0,group); pass.draw(3); pass.end();
          previous = next;
        }
        device.queue.submit([encoder.finish()]);
        images[i] = tex.createView();
      } finally { bitmap.close(); }
    }
    function solid(pixel) {
      const tex = texture({size:[1,1],format:'rgba8unorm',usage:GPUTextureUsage.TEXTURE_BINDING|GPUTextureUsage.COPY_DST});
      device.queue.writeTexture({texture:tex},new Uint8Array(pixel),{},[1,1]);
      return tex.createView();
    }
    const white = solid([255,255,255,255]), flat = solid([128,128,255,255]);
    const imageView = (info,fallback) => info ? images[gltf.textures[info.index].source] : fallback;
    const materials = gltf.materials.map((m,i) => {
      if (!used.has(i)) return null;
      const pbr = m.pbrMetallicRoughness;
      const factors = buffer(new Float32Array([...(pbr.baseColorFactor ?? [1,1,1,1]),
        pbr.metallicFactor ?? 1,pbr.roughnessFactor ?? 1,m === plate ? 0 : m.normalTexture?.scale ?? 1,
        m.extensions?.KHR_materials_transmission?.transmissionFactor ?? 0,
        Number(m.name === 'headlight_glass'),Number(m.name === 'tail_light'),
        Number(!monitor && ['aluminium.001','aloominum','Metallic','mirror'].includes(m.name)),
        monitor ? (m.name === 'monitor_screen' ? 2 : 1) : 0]),GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM);
      return device.createBindGroup({label:m.name,layout:materialLayout,entries:[
        {binding:0,resource:{buffer:factors}},{binding:1,resource:sampler},
        {binding:2,resource:imageView(pbr.baseColorTexture,white)},
        {binding:3,resource:imageView(pbr.metallicRoughnessTexture,white)},
        {binding:4,resource:imageView(m.normalTexture,flat)}]});
    });
    const opaque = [], glass = [];
    for (const draw of draws) {
      if (gltf.materials[draw.material].alphaMode === 'BLEND') {
        glass.push(draw);
        continue;
      }
      const previous = opaque.at(-1);
      if (previous?.material === draw.material && previous.first+previous.count === draw.first) {
        previous.count += draw.count;
      } else {
        opaque.push(draw);
      }
    }
    return {caseCenter:geometry.caseCenter,caseSize:geometry.caseSize,screenNormal:geometry.screenNormal,cabRatio:geometry.cabRatio,eye:geometry.eye,centerZ,headlamps,taillamps,bounds,draw(pass,pipeline,transparent,direction) {
      const parts = transparent ? glass : opaque;
      if (transparent) {
        for (const d of parts) d.depth = d.center.reduce((sum,x,i) => sum+x*direction[i],0);
        parts.sort((a,b) => b.depth-a.depth);
      }
      pass.setPipeline(pipeline);
      pass.setVertexBuffer(0,vertexBuffer); pass.setIndexBuffer(indexBuffer,'uint32');
      let bound = -1;
      for (const d of parts) {
        if (bound !== d.material) { pass.setBindGroup(1,materials[d.material]); bound = d.material; }
        pass.drawIndexed(d.count,1,d.first);
      }
    }};
  } catch (error) {
    for (const resource of allocated) resource.destroy();
    throw error;
  }
}

export function loadTaxi(device,materialLayout) {
  if (!cache.has(device)) cache.set(device,(async () => {
    const taxi = await uploadModel(device,materialLayout,assetURL);
    if (taxi) taxi.monitor = await uploadModel(device,materialLayout,monitorURL,true,taxi.cabRatio);
    return taxi;
  })().catch(error => {
    cache.delete(device);
    throw error;
  }));
  return cache.get(device);
}


────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

.\texture-filter.js
─────────────────────────────

// Shared texture-space edge interpolation and contrast-adaptive sharpening.
export const textureFilterShader = `
fn textureDifference(a:vec3f,b:vec3f) -> f32 {
  let d = a-b;
  return abs(dot(d,vec3f(0.299,0.587,0.114)))+
    0.5*abs(dot(d,vec3f(-0.169,-0.331,0.5)))+
    0.5*abs(dot(d,vec3f(0.5,-0.419,-0.081)));
}
fn textureEdge(c:array<vec3f,12>, f:vec2f, side:vec2i) -> vec3f {
  let across = textureDifference(c[0],c[4])+textureDifference(c[0],c[5])+
    textureDifference(c[3],c[7])+textureDifference(c[3],c[6])+4.0*textureDifference(c[1],c[2]);
  let along = textureDifference(c[2],c[10])+textureDifference(c[2],c[9])+
    textureDifference(c[1],c[8])+textureDifference(c[1],c[11])+4.0*textureDifference(c[0],c[3]);
  let corner = select(1.0-f,f,side==vec2i(1));
  let blend = select(clamp((corner.x+corner.y-1.0)*2.0,0.0,1.0),0.0,
    across >= along || textureDifference(c[0],c[1]) < 0.001 || textureDifference(c[0],c[2]) < 0.001);
  return mix(c[0],select(c[2],c[1],textureDifference(c[0],c[1])<=textureDifference(c[0],c[2])),blend);
}
fn textureSharpen(c:vec3f,n:vec3f,s:vec3f,w:vec3f,e:vec3f,strength:f32) -> vec3f {
  let lo = min(c,min(min(n,s),min(w,e)));
  let hi = max(c,max(max(n,s),max(w,e)));
  let gain = sqrt(clamp(min(lo,1.0-hi)/max(hi,vec3f(0.00001)),vec3f(0),vec3f(1)));
  let weight = -strength*gain/8.0;
  return clamp((c+weight*(n+s+w+e))/(1.0+4.0*weight),vec3f(0),vec3f(1));
}
fn textureLinear(c:vec3f) -> vec3f {
  return select(pow((c+0.055)/1.055,vec3f(2.4)),c/12.92,c<=vec3f(0.04045));
}
fn textureSrgb(c:vec3f) -> vec3f {
  let v = clamp(c,vec3f(0),vec3f(1));
  return select(1.055*pow(v,vec3f(1.0/2.4))-0.055,12.92*v,v<=vec3f(0.0031308));
}`;

.\vendor\truemotion1\decode.js
────────────────────────────────────────

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-2.1-or-later
// RGB555 keyframe decoder adapted from FFmpeg n8.0 libavcodec/truemotion1.c.
// Copyright (C) 2003 Alex Beregszaszi and Mike Melanson.
// https://github.com/FFmpeg/FFmpeg/blob/n8.0/libavcodec/truemotion1.c
import {vectors} from './vectors.js';

export const width = 320;
export const height = 184;
const pairsPerRow = width / 2;
const luminance = [0, -1, 1, -3, 3, -6, 6, -6];
const chrominance = [0, -1, 1, -2, 3, -4, 5, -4];
const lumaVectors = new Uint32Array(1024);
const chromaVectors = new Uint32Array(1024);
let offset = 0;
for (let base = 0; base < 1024; base += 4) {
  const count = vectors[offset++] / 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const pair = vectors[offset++];
    const high = pair >> 4;
    const low = pair & 15;
    const luma = 1057 * (luminance[high] + 65536 * luminance[low]);
    const chroma = 65537 * (1024 * chrominance[high] + chrominance[low]);
    const stop = Number(i + 1 === count);
    lumaVectors[base + i] = luma * 2 + stop;
    chromaVectors[base + i] = chroma * 2 + stop;
  }
}

// Both retail movies use the same compression mode and every frame is a
// keyframe. Keeping the two pixels packed preserves the codec's carry bits.
export function createTrueMotionDecoder() {
  const above = new Uint32Array(pairsPerRow);
  let packet, cursor, vector;

  function readIndex() {
    return cursor < packet.length ? packet[cursor++] * 4 : -1;
  }

  function delta(table) {
    if (vector < 0) throw new Error('Truncated TrueMotion vector stream.');
    const value = table[vector];
    if (!(value & 1)) {
      vector++;
      return value >>> 1;
    }
    vector = readIndex();
    if (vector) return value >>> 1;

    const wideIndex = readIndex();
    if (wideIndex < 0) {
      // The retail stream can end on the final pair's escape. Keep its
      // available delta and reject exhaustion if another pair follows.
      vector = -1;
      return value >>> 1;
    }
    const wide = table[wideIndex];
    // The zero code extends the next predictor by a factor of five.
    // Its code can itself end a vector.
    vector = wide & 1 ? readIndex() : wideIndex + 1;
    return (value >>> 1) + (wide >>> 1) * 5;
  }

  return function decode(input, output) {
    const headerSize = ((input[0] >>> 5) | (input[0] << 3)) & 127;
    const field = i => input[i + 1] ^ input[i + 2];
    if (headerSize < 12 || headerSize >= input.length ||
        field(0) !== 6 || field(1) !== 0 || field(2) !== 1 || field(10) !== 1 ||
        (field(3) | field(4) << 8) !== height || (field(5) | field(6) << 8) !== width) {
      throw new Error('Unsupported TrueMotion frame header.');
    }

    packet = input;
    cursor = headerSize;
    vector = readIndex();
    above.fill(0);
    let out = 0;
    for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
      let horizontal = 0;
      if (y % 4 === 0) {
        for (let x = 0; x < pairsPerRow; x++) {
          horizontal = (horizontal + delta(chromaVectors) + delta(lumaVectors)) | 0;
          output[out++] = above[x] += horizontal;
        }
      } else {
        for (let x = 0; x < pairsPerRow; x++) {
          horizontal = (horizontal + delta(lumaVectors)) | 0;
          output[out++] = above[x] += horizontal;
        }
      }
    }
  };
}


────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

.\vendor\truemotion1\vectors.js
─────────────────────────────────────────

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-2.1-or-later
// Vector codebook from FFmpeg n8.0 libavcodec/truemotion1data.h.
// Originally VpVision (On2), distributed with FFmpeg under the LGPL.
// https://github.com/FFmpeg/FFmpeg/blob/n8.0/libavcodec/truemotion1data.h
export const vectors = new Uint8Array([
  0x8,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00, 0x8,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00, 0x8,0x10,0x00,0x00,0x00, 0x8,0x01,0x00,0x00,0x00,
  0x8,0x00,0x10,0x00,0x00, 0x8,0x00,0x01,0x00,0x00, 0x8,0x00,0x00,0x10,0x00, 0x8,0x00,0x00,0x01,0x00,
  0x8,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x10, 0x8,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x01, 0x6,0x00,0x00,0x00, 0x6,0x10,0x00,0x00,
  0x6,0x01,0x00,0x00, 0x6,0x00,0x10,0x00, 0x6,0x00,0x01,0x00, 0x6,0x00,0x00,0x01,
  0x6,0x00,0x00,0x10, 0x6,0x00,0x00,0x02, 0x6,0x00,0x00,0x20, 0x6,0x20,0x10,0x00,
